### Page 4 — Gringotts

#### C4-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Marble and goblins. One goblin looks down at them.
- **Image:** A long marble counter, high. A goblin teller leans over it to see Hagrid, and still has to look down at Harry. Gold light, cool stone.
- **Setting:** Gringotts entrance hall. White marble, chandeliers, long counters, goblins in crimson uniforms, wizards queueing. Echoing and grand.
- **Who:** A goblin with long fingers, clever eyes, crimson coat, a quill. Hagrid, hatless, coat, a key on a chain in his palm. Harry beside him, jeans and jacket, craning up. Other wizards in robes wait behind.
- **Faces:** The goblin looks at Harry, sharp and unimpressed by fame. Harry looks up, polite and nervous. Hagrid looks at the goblin, respectful.
- **Dialogue:** **Goblin:** "Key." **Hagrid:** "Vault for Harry Potter. And one more, on the headmaster's order."
#### C4-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hagrid says worse things than goblins guard the deep vaults.
- **Image:** They walk a corridor of vault doors. Hagrid talks out of the side of his mouth. Harry’s eyes slide to a dark tunnel mouth.
- **Setting:** Underground already in feeling: bronze doors, goblin lamps, a drop in temperature. A warning plaque. The cart rails begin.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat, key, one hand on Harry’s shoulder. Harry, jacket zipped now, glasses, both hands holding the strap of nothing — he has no bag yet. A goblin walks ahead, crimson, lantern.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks ahead, casual about danger. Harry looks at the dark tunnel, swallowing, brave because he wants to be. The goblin does not look back.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Goblins are the least of what guards the deep vaults at Gringotts. Dragons, some of them."
#### C4-P2-P3

- **Beat:** A cart plunges on rails through the caves.
- **Image:** The cart tips down a waterfall of rails. Harry’s hair is straight up. Hagrid’s beard is a flag. Stalactites blur.
- **Setting:** Gringotts caverns. Underground lakes, rails like roller coasters, torch brackets, vast dark. Speed lines in the rock, not in the characters.
- **Who:** Harry gripping the cart rim, knuckles white, jacket flapping, glasses pressed to his face by the wind. Hagrid beside him, one hand lazy on the rail, grinning, coat snapping. The goblin drives, crouched, crimson coat, face into the wind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks forward, terrified and thrilled, mouth open. Hagrid looks at Harry, laughing. The goblin looks at the track, bored.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Does the cart have to go this fast?" **Hagrid:** "Best bit of Gringotts, this is."
#### C4-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Harry’s vault. A modest heap of coins. He has never owned anything.
- **Image:** A small vault, door open. Gold, silver, and bronze coins in a heap that is rich to Harry and ordinary to the bank. He stands at the edge, not stepping in, as if he might be yelled at.
- **Setting:** A round stone vault, torchlight, the cart waiting outside. No traps. Dust and shine.
- **Who:** Harry, Muggle clothes, glasses, hands held tight to his sides. Hagrid behind him, gentle, a leather pouch in one hand, ready to help him take some. The goblin holds the door.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the coins, stunned, eyes wet. He looks at Hagrid for permission. Hagrid looks at him, soft, nodding. The goblin looks at a pocket watch, impatient.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "This is mine? All of these coins?" **Hagrid:** "Your mum and dad left them. Galleons, Sickles, Knuts. Take a handful."
#### C4-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A deeper vault. Hagrid lifts a small grubby package into his coat.
- **Image:** A high-security vault, colder, the door thicker. In the middle, on a stone table, one small parcel wrapped in dirty paper and string. Hagrid’s hand closes around it. Harry is kept near the door.
- **Setting:** Deeper cavern light, blue and thin. The cart far behind. Goblin lantern. Scratches on the stone, as if something else used to be kept here.
- **Who:** Hagrid, suddenly serious, coat opened, package going into an inner pocket. Harry in the doorway, small, pouch of coins now in his hands, heavy. The goblin watches the package, not Harry.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the package, respectful and nervous. Harry looks at it, questions forming. The goblin looks at Hagrid’s pocket, professional.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "That's the parcel. Hogwarts business. Don't you touch it."
#### C4-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry asks what it is. Hagrid says it is Hogwarts business.
- **Image:** The cart climbs. Hagrid’s hand rests over the pocket. Harry twists in the seat to see his face. The package makes a square shape under the coat.
- **Setting:** The rails rising toward distant daylight. Wind. Gold sparks of other vaults far below.
- **Who:** Harry, hair wild, glasses, coin pouch in his lap, jacket. Hagrid, one big hand flat on his chest over the hidden parcel, the other on the cart. The goblin drives.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, curious, not pushy. Hagrid looks ahead, mouth shut, kind but closed. He will not tell. Harry accepts it with a small frown.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "What's in it?" **Hagrid:** "Can't say. Dumbledore's orders."

- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, the holly-and-phoenix wand comfortable in his fingers, about as long as his forearm, warm brown, no jewels. Ollivander stands back: pale silvery eyes, wispy silver hair, a dark old-fashioned suit, not wizard robes. Hands folded. Same man as the panel where he was too close.

#### C4-P4-P5

- **Setting:** Quiet shop, the same narrow room, boxes, one lamp. A feather motif is not drawn on the box. The feeling is in their faces and in the wand-light.
- **Who:** Ollivander, the same man: pale silvery eyes, wispy silver hair, dark old-fashioned suit, not robes. The wand box is in his other hand. Harry, robes, scar, glasses, the new holly wand across his palm.
- **Faces:** Ollivander’s eyes are on Harry or on the wand, curious and grave. Not on a shelf, and not glowing. Harry looks at the wand, then at Ollivander, the smile gone. He understands he is tied to the person who gave him the scar.
- **Dialogue:** **Ollivander:** "The phoenix gave one other feather, Mr Potter. It sits in the wand that scarred you. You do not choose the wand. The wand does the choosing."

- **Setting:** Diagon Alley, late light, packages piled, Hedwig’s cage, the wand box under Harry’s arm. Shoppers pass.
- **Who:** Draco, about eleven, slicked platinum hair, expensive black robes with green lining, new shoes, empty hands except the offered one, a superior tilt. An adult blond man, Lucius, is a cold silhouette further up the street, not the focus. Harry in new robes, wand box, glasses, coin pouch lighter. Hagrid, coat, trunk on his shoulder, beard, unbothered.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, smiling with no warmth, and flicks a glance of contempt at Hagrid. Harry looks at Draco, polite and already decided against him. He looks back at Hagrid with trust. Hagrid looks at Draco, mild, a little sad, not angry.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "That's Hagrid, isn't it. A servant, by the look of the coat." **Harry:** "He's a friend." **Draco:** "You'll find better company at Hogwarts, if you are the right sort."

## Chapter 5 — The Journey from Platform Nine and Three-Quarters

The barrier, Ron, Hermione, Draco again, and Hagrid’s lamp at the end of the line. First years have not been Sorted. They wear travel clothes. House colors start at the Sorting. The train is scarlet. That scarlet is the engine, not a robe.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Who:** The beige car. Harry's messy hair in the rear window. Vernon's mustache, a slice, in the front.

Petunia in the passenger seat, thin, long neck, blonde hair pinned, face set. Dudley in the back, blond and heavy, tail not visible, eating. None of them wave.

- **Who:** Harry, thin jacket over the faded castoffs, taped glasses, trolley, trunk, Hedwig, one hand starting to rise as he asks. No school robe. A platform officer in a dark station uniform and cap, mustache thinner than Vernon’s, a few steps from the barrier, about to be asked. Commuters with briefcases. The pigeon. Not Vernon. Not Molly.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the officer, then from sign to sign, lost, the hope from Diagon Alley thinning. The officer has not answered yet. Commuters do not look at him. The pigeon looks at the trolley.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Excuse me. Which way is platform nine and three-quarters?"
#### C5-P1-P3

- **Setting:** Beside that brick barrier. A train indicator clacks. People eddy around the joke. Platforms 9 and 10. No magic. No Weasleys yet.
- **Who:** The platform officer, dark uniform and cap, mustache thinner than Vernon’s, amused and dismissive. Not Vernon. Harry, trolley, thin jacket, faded castoffs, taped glasses, one hand still raised from the question. No school robe. Hedwig’s gold eye at the cloth edge.
- **Faces:** The officer looks at Harry, laughing, useless, not cruel enough to be a villain. He points away. Harry looks at the barrier, embarrassed, jaw tight. He will not ask again.
- **Dialogue:** **Guard:** "Platforms nine and ten, lad. There is no nine and three-quarters. Move along."

Percy, taller, prefect badge, tidy. Ron, younger, freckles, long nose, maroon sweater, pushing. Ginny small at Molly’s coat, not going to school yet. Harry with his trolley, watching them.
- **Faces:** Molly looks at Harry, immediate kindness. Ron looks at Harry’s scar, recognition dawning, shy. The twins look at each other, about to show off. Harry looks at the family, hope returning.
- **Dialogue:** **Molly:** "Muggles, bless them. First time to Hogwarts? You want the barrier between nine and ten."

Ron hesitating, worn maroon hand-knit with a gold R, hand-me-down jacket, rat cage on the trolley. Molly encouraging. Ginny watching, envious. Harry staring, still in the faded castoffs.
- **Faces:** The twins look back at Harry, delighted with themselves. Ron looks at the wall, nervous. Molly looks at Ron, patient love. Harry looks at the vanishing, mouth open.
- **Dialogue:** **Molly:** "Fred, George, Percy, straight at the wall, and don't stop. Ron, don't dawdle."

- **Faces:** Eyes shut, so the feeling is in his mouth: braced, brave, a little sick. Hedwig’s eye is open, calm.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "All right, Harry? Welcome to Hogwarts."

## Chapter 6 — The Sorting Hat

Boats, the hall, the Hat’s rules, the names, and Harry’s choice.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Who:** Hagrid with a lamp, huge in the moleskin coat. First years behind him in travel clothes and cloaks, not house robes.

Hermione with a book she is not reading. Neville slipping. Draco picking his way as if the mud is a personal insult. Other first years in black robes, pointed silhouettes.

- **Who:** Ron, freckles, secondhand robes, maroon sweater cuffs, dirty thumbnail, the Hat coming off in his hands as he runs. Harry still in the unsorted line, glasses, a steady presence. Hermione and Neville already at the red table, making room.
- **Faces:** On the walk, Ron looks at the stool, worried, ashamed of being scared. When the Hat shouts, the worry breaks into a grin. Harry looks at him, solid, a little smile that says he is scared too, and glad.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Not last. And not Slytherin. Please." **Sorting Hat:** "Gryffindor!"
#### C6-P3-P6

- **Beat:** “Potter, Harry.” The hall goes quiet.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s mouth open on the name. The entire hall has turned. Even the candles seem to lean. Harry is a small figure at the start of that huge attention.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall, suddenly silent. Banners, faces, the stool waiting. The quiet is drawn as empty space around him.
- **Who:** McGonagall, scroll, spectacles, emerald, looking at Harry with a flicker of something warmer than neutral. Harry, robes, messy hair, glasses, scar suddenly the point of the picture. Ron already on the red bench, half-risen. Dumbledore at the high table, watching. Snape, unblinking.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at McGonagall, then at the stool, throat tight. Hundreds of students look at him. Ron looks at him from the Gryffindor table, sympathetic, willing him over. Snape looks at him, unreadable and intense. Dumbledore looks at him, gentle.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Potter, Harry."

### Page 8 — The choice

#### C6-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The Hat covers Harry’s eyes. A voice in the brim.
- **Image:** Black inside the hat. Only a rim of candlelight at the bottom, and Harry’s mouth and scar below the brim. The leather is scratched and close.
- **Setting:** Inside the Hat. Smells of old cloth and dust. The hall is muffled.
- **Who:** Harry, the Hat down to his nose, glasses pressed, hands gripping the stool. No one else visible. The voice is a presence in the dark, not a body.
- **Faces:** His eyes are hidden. His mouth is tense. He looks into the dark of the brim as if someone is standing there.
- **Dialogue:** **Sorting Hat:** "Hmm. Difficult. Plenty of courage. And a thirst to prove yourself. Interesting."
#### C6-P4-P2

- **Beat:** It says Slytherin would make him great. Harry thinks no.
- **Image:** Split feeling in one panel. On one side, inside the dark, a green-silver vision of Harry older, colder, admired. On the other, his real mouth shapes no. He shakes his head under the hat.
- **Setting:** The stool in the silent hall, but most of the panel is the vision and the dark.
- **Who:** Harry, small hands on the stool, robes, the Hat. The vision-Harry wears finer robes, green lining, and does not look kind. Draco’s table is a distant real color.
- **Faces:** Real Harry, eyes still hidden, jaw set, refusing. The vision looks proud and alone. He does not want it. He looks, in his mind, toward the red table where Ron and Hermione are.
- **Dialogue:** **Sorting Hat:** "Slytherin would suit you, you know. Ambition. A certain disregard for rules." **Harry:** "No. Not that house. Anywhere but Slytherin."
#### C6-P4-P3

- **Beat:** A pause. Then the Hat shouts Gryffindor.
- **Image:** The Hat’s brim flies wide on the shout. Harry flinches at his own result. The word breaks the silence.
- **Setting:** The stool, candles flaring in the noise that is about to hit. Red banner behind.
- **Who:** Harry under the Hat, scar, glasses askew from the brim, both hands leaving the stool. McGonagall a step away, the corner of a smile. The Hat itself looks exerted and pleased.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are visible again as the brim lifts. He looks shocked, then flooded with relief. He looks toward Gryffindor. McGonagall looks at him, brief warmth.
- **Dialogue:** **Sorting Hat:** "Very well. Gryffindor!"
#### C6-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The red table explodes. Ron pounds the wood. The twins yell.
- **Image:** Chaos of welcome. Hands pull Harry onto the bench. Fred and George are standing on the bench yelling. Ron is pounding the table hard enough to jump the plates. Hermione is clapping, precise and happy.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor table, red and gold, spilled pumpkin juice, candles, the hall noise rushing back.
- **Who:** Harry pulled onto the bench, robes rumpled, glasses, a helpless grin. Ron already seated from his own sorting, red hair, freckles, both fists pounding the wood, juice jumping. Fred and George, identical, older, on the bench, yelling. Hermione clapping beside a space she saved. Neville beaming further down.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, pure joy, the worry gone. Harry looks at Ron, grinning, belonging. The twins look at Harry, welcoming and loud. Hermione looks at both boys, happy and right.
- **Dialogue:** **Fred:** "We've got Potter!" **George:** "About time, Ron, shove up!"
#### C6-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Dumbledore welcomes them and forbids the third-floor corridor. Ron’s laugh stops.
- **Image:** Dumbledore standing at the high table, arms open, benign, and then one finger raised on the warning. In the foreground Ron’s laugh is caught and dropped. A pumpkin juice goblet paused at his mouth.
- **Setting:** The feast beginning. Food appearing — roast, pies, piles of plates — magic of plenty. The high table. Candles.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, tall, silver beard, half-moon spectacles, purple star robes, hat. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville at the near table with full plates they have not touched. Snape further along the high table, black, watching Harry not the food. Quirrell in a purple turban, nervous, clapping a beat late.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks over the hall, kind, and the warning is light in his voice but his eyes are not joking. Ron looks at Harry, the laugh dying into a question. Harry looks at Dumbledore, snagging on “painful death.” Hermione looks thoughtful, already filing it. Snape looks at Harry.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Welcome to a new year at Hogwarts. Before the feast, one warning. The third-floor corridor, on the right-hand side, is out of bounds to all who do not wish to die a most painful death." **Ron:** "He's joking. Tell me he's joking."
#### C6-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Snape stares at Harry and does not blink.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Across the feast, through steam and candles, Snape’s face is locked on Harry. Harry has a fork halfway up and has gone still. Everyone else is eating and blurred.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall feast, warm, loud, gold. The line of sight between them is clear and cold.
- **Who:** Snape, sallow, hooked nose, greasy black shoulder-length hair, buttoned black robes, hands flat on the staff table, not eating. Harry, robes, glasses, scar, fork in hand, a roast potato on it. Ron beside him eating, out of focus, happy. Hermione talking, out of focus.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, no blink, dislike that feels personal, a thin mouth. Harry looks back, the belonging of the last panel punctured, uneasy. He does not look away first.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

## Chapter 7 — First Lessons

One place on each page: the moving stairs, then Transfiguration, then Charms, then Herbology. Picture codes such as C7-P1-P1 stay on the old jpg. A panel with no file is not drawn yet.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 2 — The moving stairs

#### C7-P1-P1

- **Beat:** A staircase moves. Harry and Ron miss their landing.
- **Image:** One entire flight turns as a single solid object. Every step stays fixed to the other steps. Both railings are part of that same flight and turn with it. The steps do not walk, shuffle, or detach. The lower end stays on its landing and is the hinge. The upper end has swung away from the landing Harry and Ron wanted, toward an empty gap, and a gap is opening between that upper end and the landing it is leaving. Harry and Ron are on the flight, almost losing balance: feet planted on the steps, bodies jerked sideways, each with a hand grabbing the railing that is turning with them, a book sliding, robes flying. They are not calmly walking. Students on the other landings have stopped and stare at the turning flight. A suit of armor watches. No other flight is moving.
- **Setting:** A tall Hogwarts stairwell, morning, high windows, stone, portraits. Camera, and use this same angle whenever a panel shows the stairs turning: three-quarter view from a landing, low enough that the bottom hinge and the top end are both visible mid-swing, with the gap opening between the moving upper end and the landing it is leaving. Not a flat head-on view. The bottom hinge is in frame, not cropped out. Only this one flight is turning. The other flights are still.
- **Who:** Harry, on the moving flight, school robes, tie, gray V-neck, glasses, feet planted, body jerked sideways, one hand locked on the turning railing, book bag slipping. Ron, on the same flight, red hair, secondhand robes, maroon sweater, feet planted, body jerked sideways, one hand on that same railing, a book sliding. Students on the landings, stopped, faces turned toward the flight, nobody else riding it. A suit of armor, empty, spear in its gauntlet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the departing landing, alarmed, almost falling. Ron looks at Harry, a wild grin that is also panic, off balance. The students on the landings stare at the turning flight. The armor looks, somehow, unimpressed.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "That was our landing!" **Harry:** "It moved. The whole staircase moved."
#### C7-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A portrait scolds them for being lost.
- **Image:** A painted wizard in a ruff leans out of his frame, finger wagging. They have stopped on a landing that is the wrong century of the castle.
- **Setting:** A corridor of portraits, morning light, a tapestry, a window onto the grounds far below. They are high up.
- **Who:** The portrait, an old wizard, painted robes, angry little beard, leaning out so far his frame cuts him at the waist. Harry and Ron, books clutched, out of breath, ties crooked.
- **Faces:** The portrait looks down his nose at them, scolding. Harry looks up, polite, lost. Ron looks at the portrait, offended at being told off by paint.
- **Dialogue:** **Portrait:** "Lost, are you? First years always are. The castle does not rearrange itself for your convenience. Except when it does."

- **Dialogue:** **Peeves:** "New blood, new blood! Peeves has a present for the ickle firsties!"

## Chapter 8 — The Potions Master

The same day, after the classrooms. Four scenes, one place each: the way down, the dungeon before the questions, the humiliation, and the corridor after. Old pictures keep their old codes and files.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Setting:** The castle corridor above the dungeons. Afternoon.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione. Other students ahead, smaller, descending.

- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, soft and mean. Harry looks back, wary. Hermione looks at her own lowering hand, flushed. Draco looks at Harry, enjoying it.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "Mr Potter. Fame has walked into my classroom. Do stand, if you can spare us the attention."

### Page 4 — The humiliation

#### C7-P3-P4

- **Beat:** A potions question. Harry says he does not know.
- **Image:** Snape looms at the end of their table. Harry’s mouth has just said the honest answer. The parchment is still blank. Hermione is vibrating with the answer.
- **Setting:** Close on their table: cauldron, crushed herbs they have not touched, torch shadow of Snape’s nose across the wood.
- **Who:** Snape, black, both hands on the table, leaning in. Harry seated, robes, glasses, empty hands turned up. Hermione beside him, lips pressed, hand twitching and staying down because she was ignored. Ron staring at his quill.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, contempt. Harry looks at him, not defiant yet, simply without the answer, embarrassed. Hermione looks at Snape, frustrated. Ron looks at Harry, sympathetic and useless.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "Asphodel root, powdered, in an infusion of wormwood. Name the draught, Mr Potter." **Harry:** "I don't know, sir." **Snape:** "A bezoar, then. Where does one find it?" **Harry:** "I don't know, sir."
#### C7-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione waves harder. Draco smirks.
- **Image:** Hermione’s hand is up again, higher, her book bouncing. Draco is a pale profile of amusement. Snape does not turn his head one degree toward her.
- **Setting:** The two tables. Torches. The class is a held breath.
- **Who:** Hermione, half out of her seat, robes, hair, arm aching upward. Draco, platinum hair, perfect posture, a smirk, quill tapping. Snape, back partly to her, face to Harry. Harry wishing the floor would take him.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Snape, desperate to be fair to the answer. Draco looks at Harry, smug. Snape looks only at Harry. Harry looks at the blank parchment, jaw tight.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Sir, asphodel and wormwood make the Draught of Living Death. A bezoar comes from the stomach of a goat. It cures most poisons." **Draco:** "Famous, and he can't read a label."
#### C7-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Snape asks again, softer and meaner. Harry’s scar prickles.
- **Image:** Extreme close. Snape’s mouth, quiet. Harry’s scar, a tiny flare of discomfort, his fingers touching it under the table where Snape cannot see. Sweat at his hairline.
- **Setting:** Torchlight, black robes filling the background, the dungeon gone abstract.
- **Who:** Snape’s face, greasy hair, hooked nose, black eyes, no blink. Harry’s face, glasses, scar, a hand below frame on the scar. No one else.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at the scar itself, not only the boy, intense. Harry looks at Snape’s eyes and does not understand the hatred. Pain is small and private on his brow.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "Monkshood and wolfsbane, Mr Potter. The difference, if you please." **Harry:** "I don't know, sir." **Snape:** "There is none. They are the same plant. Also called aconite. How disappointing."
#### C7-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Harry says Hermione knows. Snape takes points for arrogance.
- **Image:** Harry points his chin toward Hermione, giving her the answer. Snape’s hand flicks as if removing something from the air: points, gone. Hermione looks stricken that helping has made it worse.
- **Setting:** The dungeon table. A house-point hourglass is not in the room; the loss is in their faces. Torches.
- **Who:** Harry, half-standing, robes, one hand indicating Hermione. Hermione, hand finally down, hurt. Snape, black, a cold small smile, straightening. Ron furious and silent.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hermione, apologetic, then at Snape, confused. Snape looks at Harry, satisfied, calling it arrogance. Hermione looks at Harry, grateful and dismayed. Ron looks at Snape, hate.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hermione knows the answer, sir. Ask her." **Snape:** "Arrogance, Mr Potter. Five points from Gryffindor, for hiding behind a classmate."
#### C7-P4-P2

- **Beat:** More points, because Harry could not answer.
- **Image:** Snape turns his back, robes flaring, and the loss is a second flick of his fingers. Draco mouths the number, savoring it. Harry sits down hard.
- **Setting:** The dungeon. Slytherin table relaxed. Gryffindor table small.
- **Who:** Snape walking to the blackboard, black hair, black robes, chalk in hand now, done with them. Draco, Crabbe, Goyle pleased. Harry seated, glasses, blank parchment, fists under the table. Ron and Hermione on either side.
- **Faces:** Snape does not look back. Draco looks at Harry, victorious. Harry looks at his fists. He is angry now, not just embarrassed. Hermione looks at the blackboard, blinking hard.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "And five more, because you knew nothing at all. Sit down, Mr Potter."
#### C7-P4-P3

- **Beat:** The dislike looks personal.
- **Image:** A close profile of Snape at the board. He is writing ingredients. His eye, though, is slid sideways, still on Harry. This is not about a wrong answer.
- **Setting:** Blackboard, white chalk words, torch shadow, jars.
- **Who:** Snape only, sallow cheek, hooked nose, greasy hair, black collar. Harry is a soft shape in the eye’s direction, out of focus.
- **Faces:** Snape’s mouth is neutral. His eye is not. He looks at Harry with an old grievance Harry has not earned this week.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

## Chapter 9 — Flying class and the Seeker

Brooms, the theft, the dive, and a place on the team.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Setting:** A sunny corridor corner. Armor. A window. The end of the morning.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, grinning, Remembrall raised, glasses, scar, robes. Ron, red hair, freckles, both fists up, shouting. Hermione, books, hair, one hand over her smile, the other hitting Harry’s arm lightly for scaring her.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, proud and disbelieving, the sky still in his eyes. Ron looks at him, joy. Hermione looks at him, fond and exasperated, the expulsion fear leaving. The ball looks tiny and important between them.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I'm on the Gryffindor Quidditch team." **Ron:** "You're what?" **Hermione:** "You were supposed to be expelled."

## Chapter 10 — The Midnight Duel

A trap, a chase, Fluffy, and the memory of a package.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

## Chapter 11 — Hallowe’en

An insult, a feast, a troll, a lie, and three friends.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 4 — The announcement

#### C10-P2-P1

- **Who:** Quirrell, purple robes, large purple turban askew so a pale scalp shows at the edge, stammer visible in his hands. Dumbledore rising, silver beard, half-moon spectacles. McGonagall standing, emerald. Snape standing, black, eyes sharp. Students twisted around. Harry and Ron on their feet.

- **Setting:** The hall in panic. Pumpkins, spilled juice, bats overhead, candles swinging.
- **Who:** Quirrell on the floor, turban still on, eyes shut, arms spread. Students standing on benches. Draco calm and annoyed, not scared, Crabbe and Goyle bumping people. Harry and Ron braced against the flow. Hermione is not here.

- **Setting:** The hall, mess and order fighting. House colors gathering.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple star robes, beard, spectacles, commanding and kind. Percy Weasley, red hair, prefect badge, organizing Gryffindor, serious. McGonagall directing. ### Page 9 — The lie

#### C10-P4-P1

- **Beat:** McGonagall, Snape, and Quirrell burst in. McGonagall is white with anger.
- **Image:** The door fills with three teachers. McGonagall in front, face bloodless. Snape behind, eyes raking the room. Quirrell peering, turban straight again, handkerchief at his nose.

- **Who:** McGonagall, emerald robes, bun coming loose, spectacles, no hat, fury. Snape, black, greasy hair, a limp he is hiding, one leg stiff. Quirrell, purple, turban, trembling. Harry, Ron, Hermione in a line, dripping.

- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Hermione, the anger pausing. Hermione looks up at her, determined, about to lie for them. Harry looks at the back of Hermione’s head, startled. Ron looks at her, guilty that she is doing this.

#### C10-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says she went looking for the troll, and the boys came to save her.
- **Image:** Hermione, hands spread, selling the lie with a steady voice. She does not look at the boys, because she might laugh or cry. McGonagall’s face shifts from fury to a different disappointment.
- **Setting:** Close three-shot. The bathroom soft behind. Water drips off Hermione’s hair.
- **Who:** Hermione, soaked robes, ruined book forgotten, empty hands, the lie held carefully. McGonagall listening, spectacles a little fogged. Harry and Ron statues behind, not contradicting her.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at McGonagall, earnest, brave in a new way. A flicker of fear she will not be believed. McGonagall looks at her, reading it, choosing to allow it. Harry looks at the floor, honoring the lie by silence.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I went looking for the troll. I thought I could manage it. They came to stop me being killed. The fault is mine."
#### C10-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Points off Hermione, points to the boys. Snape looks unconvinced.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s hands move like a scale: a deduction toward Hermione, awards toward the boys. Snape’s mouth is a cut. He does not clap. Quirrell does, weakly, and looks at Snape.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the bathroom now, the door shut on the troll. Torches. Wet footprints.
- **Who:** McGonagall, composed again, emerald, delivering the points. Hermione, punished and proud. Harry and Ron, uncomfortable with being rewarded. Snape, black, weight on his good leg, eyes on Harry. Quirrell, turban, hands fluttering.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Hermione, stern affection. Hermione looks back, accepting the points loss. Snape looks at Harry, he does not believe a word, and he looks at Quirrell once, colder. Quirrell looks at the floor. Harry looks at Snape’s leg.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Five points from Gryffindor, Miss Granger, for foolishness. Five points each to Potter and Weasley, for sheer nerve." **Snape:** "How very convenient a story."
#### C10-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Leaving, Harry sees blood soaking through Snape’s trouser leg.
- **Image:** As Snape turns, his black robe swings and shows a dark wet patch at the calf, too red to be water. Harry’s eyes lock on it. Snape’s hand twitches the robe closed a second late.
- **Setting:** The torchlit corridor. The others walking ahead. A private piece of seeing.
- **Who:** Snape from behind and side, black robes, greasy hair, the injured leg, one hand catching the cloth. Harry a step back, glasses, scar, wet, staring. Ron and Hermione ahead, not seeing. Quirrell far, turban bobbing.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the blood, suspicion clicking into place, mouth closed. Snape does not look back, but his shoulders know he was seen. His profile, if caught, is pain held very still.

#### C10-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Later the three sit together. Thanks, muttered both ways. They are friends.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The common room, late, almost empty. Three chairs pulled close to the fire. Hermione has a towel on her hair. Ron is saying thank you into his pumpkin juice. She is saying it back into her knees. Harry watches them and the fire, and the group is a triangle that will hold.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, night, fire, Halloween over. Quiet. A clock. Red hangings.
- **Who:** Hermione, towel, dry robes, bushy hair escaping, a mug. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, ears still red, a mug. Harry, glasses, scar, dry, a blanket on his shoulders, mug in both hands. No one else.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his mug, then at Hermione, shy thanks. Hermione looks at the fire, then at Ron, the insult forgiven if not forgotten, a small smile. Harry looks at both of them, content, the suspicion about Snape set aside for this one warm panel. They look like friends.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Thanks. For the lie." **Hermione:** "Thanks for the troll. Friends, then."

## Chapter 12 — Quidditch

Nerves, a cursed broom, a small fire, the Snitch, and a name Hagrid did not mean to say.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Setting:** Outside the stadium, match day.
- **Who:** Harry in Gryffindor Quidditch robes, too long. The crowd, all four houses. Not a red-only crowd.
- **Faces:** He looks swallowed by the robe and the noise.

### Page 2 — Before the whistle

#### C11-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The changing room. Harry’s robes are too big. Wood kneels with the plan.
- **Image:** A cramped stone changing room. Harry stands in Quidditch robes that pool at his shoes. Wood kneels, drawing the plan on a bench with a wet finger. The team is a wall of older bodies.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor changing room. Benches, pads, a high window, the roar of a crowd already outside, muffled. Red and gold everywhere.
- **Who:** Harry, small, messy hair, glasses, scar, scarlet Quidditch robes too long, gloves too big, a broom — a clean fast one, Nimbus — beside him. Oliver Wood, brown hair, captain’s badge, arm guard, kneeling. The twins Fred and George in Beater gear, identical grins. Other teammates, Alicia, Katie, Angelina, older, kind, tall.
- **Faces:** Wood looks up at Harry, intense, trying to pour a whole game into him. Harry looks at the finger-plan, swallowing, brave and new. Fred looks at George, a reassuring joke. They look at Harry, friendly.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Chasers mind the Quaffle. Beaters mind the Bludgers. You, Potter, watch the sky for the Snitch and nothing else."
#### C11-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Catch the Snitch and they cannot lose. Wood’s eyes are not sure a first year can.
- **Image:** Close. Wood’s hands on Harry’s shoulders, steadying the too-big robes. His mouth says the simple plan. His eyes flick to Harry’s size and back, hiding the doubt badly.
- **Setting:** The bench, the roar nearer. A whistle somewhere. Leather and grass smell at the door.
- **Who:** Wood, captain, brown hair, hands on the small shoulders. Harry, gloves, glasses, scar, the broom now in his grip.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at Harry, willing him to be taller. Harry looks back, he heard the doubt and he nods anyway. A tight brave smile.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Catch the Golden Snitch and they cannot catch our score. I am trusting a first year, and a Nimbus Two Thousand. Do not make me regret either."
#### C11-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Harry walks out. The stadium is a roar.
- **Image:** The tunnel mouth. Dark, then a blast of light and sound. Harry is a silhouette becoming a small scarlet figure. The stands are a moving texture of houses.
- **Setting:** The Quidditch pitch, daytime, packed wooden stands, banners, the castle beyond, a bright cold sky. Four goal hoops at each end.
- **Who:** Harry stepping out, too-big robes, broom in hand, glasses, scar, gloves. Wood ahead of him. The crowd, including Ron and Hermione in the Gryffindor stand, tiny. Hagrid huge even at a distance, a flower behind his ear or just his beard. McGonagall in the staff seats. Snape. Quirrell’s turban.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up at the roar, eyes wide, a step hitching then landing. He looks at the sky, which he trusts more than the crowd. Wood looks back, a nod.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C11-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Green team below. Draco points from the stands.
- **Image:** Slytherin in green, already mounted, looking expensive and mean. In the stands Draco is on his feet, arm out, pointing at Harry so the people around him will look and laugh.
- **Setting:** The pitch and the Slytherin section. Green and silver banners. The grass marked.
- **Who:** Slytherin players, green robes, brooms, a larger Seeker sneering. Draco, platinum hair, school robes, pointing, Crabbe and Goyle laughing beside him. Harry small on the pitch, looking up by accident.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, mocking, mouth open in a shout Harry cannot hear. The Slytherin Seeker looks at Harry, dismissing him. Harry looks at Draco for one second, then away, at his broom. He will not play to the point.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Look at the scarf on legs! Gryffindor's Seeker still needs a growth charm!"

- **Beat:** Hooch throws the balls up. Harry kicks off.
- **Image:** Madam Hooch’s arm flings the Quaffle. Two Bludgers and the tiny gold Snitch blast upward. Harry kicks off in the same instant, a clean rise. The teams follow.
- **Setting:** The center circle. Bright grass. The sky opening. Balls like exclamation points.
- **Who:** Madam Hooch, yellow eyes, whistle in her teeth, dark cloak, arm extended. Harry rising, scarlet, broom, body remembering the courtyard. Wood shouting. Fred and George up with bats. The Snitch a gold speck already gone.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at the balls, fierce and fair. Harry looks up, the fear burned off, focus arriving. His mouth is slightly open. Joy under the job.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Mount your brooms. Three, two, one, brooms up!"
#### C11-P1-P6

- **Beat:** From above, the pitch is a diagram and Harry is a speck.
- **Image:** Bird’s eye. The pitch is Wood’s diagram come alive: hoops, tiny players, the crowd a ring. Harry is one scarlet speck circling higher than the game.
- **Setting:** Daytime stadium, castle, lake, the Forbidden Forest a dark edge. Wind, implied by the lean of banners.
- **Who:** Everyone tiny. Harry’s speck apart from the knot of Chasers. The Snitch not visible, which is the point. Snape’s black speck in the staff stand. Hermione’s speck already standing up.
- **Faces:** None readable. The picture is scale. Harry is small and the sky is his.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 3 — The jinx

#### C11-P2-P1

- **Beat:** He hunts for a glint of gold. A Bludger screams past.
- **Image:** Harry banks. A black iron Bludger tears past his ear, a blur with a face like a cannonball. He does not chase it. His eyes search the air for gold.
- **Setting:** Mid-air. The crowd a smear. A goal hoop. Sun in his eyes.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet robes streaming, gloves, glasses, broom, body low over the handle. The Bludger. George or Fred far below, bat swinging, red hair.
- **Faces:** Harry looks past the Bludger, not at it, hunting. Concentration. A flicker of thrill. The Beater looks up at him, protective.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C11-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The broom bucks. He clamps his knees.
- **Image:** The broom jerks like a living thing bitten. Harry’s knees lock. One hand stays on, the other flies up. His glasses slip.
- **Setting:** Open air above the center. The game continuing below, not seeing him yet.
- **Who:** Harry, small on a suddenly wild broom, scarlet cloth, messy hair, glasses crooked, scar. The broom, fine wood, misbehaving.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the broom, startled, then at his own hands. The joy is gone. Confusion first, then the start of fear.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Steady. What are you doing? Steady!"
#### C11-P2-P3

- **Beat:** It twists harder. One hand slips off.
- **Image:** He is sideways. One hand is off. The broom rolls. The ground is a green threat. The crowd has seen now; a thousand faces tilt up.
- **Setting:** High above the pitch. Banners. A collective gasp drawn as open mouths in the stands, small.
- **Who:** Harry hanging half-off, one glove locked, the other grabbing air, robes over his head partly, glasses barely on. Wood below, looking up, the game forgotten. Hooch’s whistle frozen.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ground, real fear, teeth set, not screaming. Wood looks up, horrified. He looks at the teachers, wanting a stop.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Potter's in trouble! The broom's fighting him!"
#### C11-P2-P4

- **Beat:** In the stands, Hermione tracks the magic with her eyes.
- **Image:** Hermione is standing on the bench, hair whipped by wind, eyes narrowed, following an invisible line from the air down to the staff seats. Ron is pulling her sleeve, panicked about Harry, not about clues.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor stand. Scarves, shouting, Hagrid a few rows away, huge, upset, fists clenched. The pitch far below in the angle of the panel, Harry a tumbling speck.
- **Who:** Hermione, school robes, red-and-gold scarf, binoculars in one hand she is not using because she is looking at people, not the broom. Ron, scarf, freckles, white-faced, both hands on the rail. Hagrid, moleskin coat, devastated.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the staff, detective-sharp, scared. Ron looks only at Harry, begging the air. Hagrid looks at Harry, helpless, eyes wet.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's a jinx. Someone is jinxing his broom." **Ron:** "Then find them, don't explain them!"
#### C11-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Snape is motionless, staring at Harry, mouth moving.
- **Image:** The staff stand, still amid screaming people. Snape sits rigid. His eyes are locked upward. His lips move. One hand is hidden in his robe. McGonagall beside him is standing, shouting for order. He does not stand.
- **Setting:** The teachers’ box. Benches, a railing, the pitch beyond. Quirrell is there too, turban, seemingly fidgeting, easy to miss because Snape is so still.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, greasy hair, sallow face, unmoving body, moving mouth. McGonagall, emerald, on her feet, spectacles, alarmed. Quirrell, purple turban, hands in his lap, also looking up, also mouthing, overlooked. Dumbledore is not in this crop.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, intense, no blink. It reads as an attack. McGonagall looks at the sky, afraid for the boy. Quirrell looks up, a bead of sweat, his stare the true one if anyone were looking at him. No one is.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "Hold fast. Contra the jinx. Do not throw him."
#### C11-P2-P6

- **Beat:** She shoves through the crowd toward the staff seats.
- **Image:** Hermione is a determined wedge through scarves and elbows. Ron is behind her, confused, still looking back at the sky. She does not explain. Her wand is already in her fist.
- **Setting:** The packed stand, stairs, house colors, the staff box getting closer. Noise.
- **Who:** Hermione, scarf flying, bushy hair, wand out, face set. Ron stumbling after, red hair. Spectators turning, annoyed. Hagrid shifting his bulk to let her pass, trusting her without knowing why.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Snape’s box, fierce. Ron looks at her, lost, then up at Harry, torn. A spectator looks at her wand, alarmed. Hagrid looks at her, hopeful.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's Snape. He's staring at Harry and his mouth hasn't stopped. Come on."

### Page 4 — Fire and the Snitch

#### C11-P3-P1

- **Beat:** A small blue flame catches Snape’s hem.
- **Image:** Hermione leans over a railing and a tiny precise blue fire bites the hem of Snape’s black robe. It is small and rude and perfect. His concentration snaps.
- **Setting:** The edge of the staff seats. Wood railing. The crowd behind Hermione. Snape’s robes a black pool.
- **Who:** Hermione, wand tip glowing, scarf, determination. Snape, seated, the flame on his hem, eyes breaking from the sky. Ron behind her, suddenly understanding, a gasp. Quirrell flinching as the counter-jinx — unseen — dies, a sweat drop, not noticed.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the flame, not gloating, urgent. Snape looks down, shock and fury. Ron looks at the fire, horrified and impressed. Harry is not in frame. The sky is winning off-panel.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Lacarnum inflamari."
#### C11-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Snape beats the robe. The broom goes still.
- **Image:** Split feeling: Snape smacking his hem with both hands, standing, black hair falling; and far off, Harry’s broom leveling so suddenly he almost floats.
- **Setting:** Staff box and open air, one panel with a clear distance between them.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, smoking hem, furious hands. McGonagall looking at the hem, suspicious of Hermione more than of him. Harry in the air, one hand on, body unfolding back onto the broom. Hermione ducking below the railing.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at his robe, then snaps his head toward the stands, rage. Harry looks at the broom, a sob of relief that is not quite a smile. Hermione looks up from hiding, checking him. McGonagall looks at the spot Hermione was, eyes narrowed.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "My robes! Who set fire to my robes?"
#### C11-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry hangs one-handed. Something gold brushes his lips.
- **Image:** He is still recovering, hanging, and the Snitch — tiny, gold, silver wings — flicks against his mouth as if choosing him. His eyes cross trying to see it.
- **Setting:** Air, sun, the gold insect-bright against his face. The pitch far below, game resumed in miniature.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet, glasses, one glove on the broom, the other hand too far, the Snitch at his lips. No one else near. Seekers are supposed to chase. This one arrives.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the Snitch, cross-eyed, astonishment. His mouth opens on instinct. The Snitch is all wings and will.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

- **Beat:** He hits the grass. The crowd thinks he has fallen.
- **Image:** A thud of scarlet on the grass. The broom beside him. The crowd is on its feet in the wrong kind of silence. Dust. He is curled, both hands at his mouth.
- **Setting:** The pitch, center, bright day. Players landing. Wood running. The stands a wall of alarm.
- **Who:** Harry on the ground, robes muddy now as well as too big, glasses askew, hands over his mouth. Wood running, captain’s badge, fear. Hooch approaching. The Slytherin Seeker circling, confused.
- **Faces:** The crowd looks down, afraid. Wood looks at Harry, panicked. Harry’s eyes are open, looking up at them, and there is a secret in his cheeks. He has not shown it.
- **Dialogue:** **Lee Jordan:** "Potter's down! Gryffindor's Seeker is down!"
#### C11-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He pulls the Snitch out of his mouth.
- **Image:** Close. Mud, a grin, and the golden Snitch held up between two fingers, wings beating against his pinch. His lip is cut a little. It is perfect.
- **Setting:** Grass and sky. The roar about to return. Sun on gold.
- **Who:** Harry, filthy scarlet, glasses, scar, hair full of grass, the Snitch in his fingers. His other hand braces him up.
- **Faces:** He looks at the Snitch, then past it at the stands, a dazed laugh. Triumph, disbelief, a child’s joy. The Snitch looks caught and furious, wings blurring.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I've got it. I've got the Snitch!"
#### C11-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Gryffindor erupts. Wood lifts him. Harry is dazed and laughing.
- **Image:** Wood hoists him. The twins crash in. The stand is a red explosion. Scarves. Hermione and Ron screaming. Hagrid pounding his knees. The Snitch still in Harry’s fist above the tangle.
- **Setting:** The pitch and the Gryffindor stand, one celebration across the panel. Banners. Daylight.
- **Who:** Harry lifted, muddy, laughing, Snitch held high. Wood, brown hair, yelling. Fred and George, bats dropped. Ron and Hermione in the stand, scarves, faces open. Hagrid roaring with happiness. Far in the staff box, Snape sitting again, hem singed, not cheering. Draco on his feet, not cheering.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron and Hermione, laughing, finding them in the crowd. Wood looks at Harry, the doubt gone, delighted. Snape looks at Harry, cold, the corner of his mouth tight. Draco looks at the Snitch, sour.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Seeker! You mad, beautiful Seeker!" **Ron:** "He caught it in his mouth!"

### Page 6 — The name

#### C11-P4-P1

- **Who:** Hagrid, coat off, a huge knit vest, beard, pouring tea from a kettle like a cauldron. Harry, school robes, a bruise of a grin still, glasses, scar, mug in both hands. Ron, freckles, maroon sweater, eyeing a rock cake with respect. Hermione, scarf off, books even here, mug. Fang, a black boarhound, head on paws.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, proud, misty. Harry looks at the tea, happy and tired. Ron looks at the cake, wary. Hermione looks at Hagrid, fond, and already steering toward a question.

- **Setting:** The table, firelight, Fang’s ear twitching.
- **Who:** Harry, earnest, glasses reflecting fire. Hagrid, vest, beard, dismissive gentle hand. Hermione, sharp. Ron nodding with Harry, loyal to the theory.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, sure, a little hurt that he is not believed. Hagrid looks at Harry, troubled but firm, he likes Snape enough to doubt this, or he fears the subject. Ron looks at Hagrid, disagreeing. Hermione looks at Hagrid, noticing what he will not say.

- **Who:** Hermione, hands demonstrating. Hagrid, back half-turned, kettle, shoulders up. Harry watching the window because Hagrid did. Ron mid-bite of a rock cake, regretting it.

#### C11-P4-P4

- **Setting:** The hut, fire flaring, mugs jumped.
- **Who:** Hagrid, vest, beard, both hands up as if he could push the sentence back into Harry’s mouth. Harry seated, calm now, glasses, the question still in the air. Hermione very still, listening. Ron frozen with the cake. Fang, drool, alert.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, panic, affection, fear of having said too much even by reacting. Harry looks at him, gentle and relentless. Hermione looks at Hagrid, kind, waiting. Fang looks at the door, missing the point.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I saw you take a parcel from a high-security vault at Gringotts. That's what the dog is standing on, isn't it?" **Hagrid:** "Harry!"
#### C11-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Defending the secret, Hagrid says Nicolas Flamel, then claps a hand over his mouth.
- **Image:** The name escapes. Hagrid’s eyes go round. His huge hand slaps over his beard and mouth, too late. The children are three statues of attention.
- **Setting:** Tight on the table. Firelight. The name is the brightest thing, though it is not written in giant letters; it is in their faces.
- **Who:** Hagrid, hand over his mouth, vest, horrified at himself. Harry, Ron, Hermione, all leaning in, mugs forgotten. Fang back asleep, a comedic heap.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them over his hand, pleading with them to un-hear. Harry looks at him, the name locked in. Hermione looks at Hagrid, already spelling it in her head, eyes bright. Ron looks at Harry, a silent we-have-it.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "It's Nicolas Flamel's business, that's all, and I should never have... oh, blast my big mouth."
#### C11-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Three faces. Hermione is already memorizing the spelling.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Outside the hut, later. The door shuts on Hagrid’s worried face in the window. On the path, the three walk. Hermione’s finger writes the name on her palm in the dark, invisible ink of memory. Harry and Ron flank her, looking at her hand.
- **Setting:** The path to the castle, night, cold, the hut’s window warm behind them, the forest black to one side, the castle ahead.
- **Who:** Hermione, scarf, robes, one palm up, finger tracing letters. Harry, glasses, scar, hands in his pockets, thoughtful. Ron, sweater, breath clouding, excited. Hagrid small in the window behind, hand still near his mouth.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her palm, concentrated, a scholar on a hunt. Harry looks at her, trusting her with the name. Ron looks at the castle, eager and scared. Hagrid looks at their backs, regret and love.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Nicolas Flamel. I'm spelling it until it sticks."

## Chapter 13 — Nicolas Flamel

Searching, the book, what the Stone does, and the wrong man watched.

### Page 2 — The search

#### C12-P1-P1

- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, a list of crossed-out names. Ron, head on his hand, freckles, maroon sweater, bored and loyal. Hermione, surrounded by open books, quill, bushy hair, the engine of the search. Madam Pince is not in this panel yet, only her desk far off.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at a page, tireless. Harry looks at a book, hopeful and fading. Ron looks at Harry, a silent how-long. The shelves look down, indifferent.

#### C12-P1-P2

- **Setting:** Green lamp light. The table. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hand, ink, a bitten nail she would deny. Harry’s face faint in the wood, glasses. No one else needed.
- **Faces:** Her finger is the expression: precise, then a small defeated tap. Harry looks at the empty line, mouth tight.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Nothing under Flamel. Nothing under Stone."

- **Setting:** The library aisle. Her domain. A cart of books.

- **Setting:** The table, lamps, the restricted rope in the dark. Quiet comedy.
- **Who:** Ron asleep, freckles, sweater, mouth open. Harry, glasses, a small smile, quill as a poke. Hermione, still reading, a stack to her eyebrow.
- **Faces:** Ron’s face is slack, peaceful, a dream of food maybe. Harry looks at him, fond. Hermione looks at her page, a tiny smile she will not admit, she heard the poke.

- **Setting:** Hall, classroom, library. Days passing by the change of light and their heavier eyes.

- **Faces:** Harry looks more tired and more fixed. Ron looks loyal and sick of books. Hermione looks undefeated. Snape looks at his plate, ordinary, which frustrates Harry.

### Page 4 — The book

#### C12-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione slams down a huge book she checked out weeks ago for fun.

- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, evening, fire, homework chaos. Red hangings.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair bigger than usual, robes, both hands on the book, proud. Harry leaning in, glasses, scar. Ron on the floor chasing a chess knight that is kicking him. The book, leather, old, a title about alchemy too worn to shout.

- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I had it out for light reading weeks ago. Move the chess pieces."
#### C12-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Her finger finds the name.
- **Image:** The page. Her ink-stained finger underlines Nicolas Flamel in a sea of type. A small engraved portrait of an old man beside it. The name is finally real.
- **Setting:** Firelight on paper. The common-room table. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hand. Harry’s hand at the margin, not touching the name, reverent. Ron’s freckled finger pointing from the other side, a second late.
- **Faces:** Only partial. Hermione’s mouth at the top, a satisfied line. Harry’s eyes in the shine of his glasses at the edge, wide. They all look at the name.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "There. Nicolas Flamel."
#### C12-P2-P3

- **Beat:** All three lean in. Firelight on the page.
- **Image:** Three heads in a triangle over the book. Hair: black, red, brown. The fire makes the page gold. The rest of the common room does not exist.
- **Setting:** Close, warm, the book a campfire.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses sliding down, scar, robes. Ron, freckles, a smudge of chess-piece dirt. Hermione, bushy hair tickling both of them, she does not notice.
- **Faces:** They look down, breathless, together. Harry looks hungry for the answer. Hermione looks like a guide. Ron looks like he might finally enjoy a book.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Go on, then. Read it out."
#### C12-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Flamel is centuries old because of one object.
- **Image:** Hermione reads, finger moving. On the page, a suggestion of a long life: the engraved old man, and a smaller note of a date that is impossible. Their faces age with the idea, not literally.
- **Setting:** The same table. A clock. Other students laughing far away, ordinary.
- **Who:** The three, as before. The book doing the talking. Hermione’s mouth moving. Harry very still. Ron’s eyebrows up.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the page, awed by the fact. Harry looks at the old engraved face, thinking of someone who does not want to die. Ron looks at Harry, the joke of treasure arriving, then fading into seriousness.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "He is hundreds of years old. The only reason he is still alive is one object."
#### C12-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A picture of a small red stone.
- **Image:** The engraving fills the panel. A small, irregular, deep red stone, drawn simply, glowing in the ink. It is not large. It does not look like it should hold a war. That is why it is frightening.
- **Setting:** The page, firelight, their thumbs at the corners holding the book open.
- **Who:** No full bodies. Three thumbs. The stone.
- **Faces:** None. The stone is the face of the chapter. It looks inert and priceless.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C12-P2-P6

- **Image:** He sits back. The common room returns. He looks at the fire, but he is seeing Fluffy’s paws and the trapdoor. His hand is flat on the picture of the stone.
- **Setting:** The common room, evening, friends around, the book open.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, one hand on the red engraving. Ron and Hermione watching him, the conclusion landing on them a second later. A chess piece tugging Ron’s sock, ignored.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the fire, certain, uneasy. Hermione looks at him, connecting dog, package, stone, nodding slowly. Ron looks at the book, then at the ceiling, toward the third floor, a silent oh.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "That's what Fluffy is standing on. The Philosopher's Stone."

### Page 5 — What the Stone does

#### C12-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione explains gold, and a potion that keeps you alive.
- **Image:** She holds the book like a teacher, two fingers up: gold, life. The fire paints the red stone’s color onto her hand. The boys listen without jokes.
- **Setting:** The common room, closer to the fire now, the book on Harry’s knees.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, the book. Harry and Ron on the hearthrug, cross-legged, mugs. Other students out of focus, playing exploding snap, ordinary children.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, grave, making sure they understand the size of it. Harry looks at her fingers, then at the scar in the shine of a mug. Ron looks at her, the poor-boy understanding of endless gold flickering, then shame at thinking it.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "The Philosopher's Stone turns metal to gold, and it makes the Elixir of Life. Drink that, and you do not die."
#### C12-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Ron says that would end being poor and end being dead.
- **Image:** Ron says it simply, no greed in the drawing, just a boy who owns one rat and a lot of brothers. His hand is open, empty. Harry looks at that empty hand.
- **Setting:** The hearthrug. Fire. A hole in Ron’s sock, visible, not mocked.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, red hair, open hand. Harry beside him, quiet. Hermione with the book closed now, respecting the sentence.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the fire, matter-of-fact, a little sad. Harry looks at Ron, affection, he will not tease this. Hermione looks at Ron, soft, the nightmare comment from Halloween fully buried.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Gold, and not dying. If you had that, you'd never be poor, and you'd never be gone."
#### C12-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry is quiet, thinking of his parents.
- **Image:** He is in the chair, small. The fire becomes, just for him, a soft not-quite-memory: a woman with kind eyes, a man with glasses, too vague to be a picture, more a warmth. He does not tell the others yet.
- **Setting:** The common room, but the edges fade. Firelight.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, knees up, mug forgotten. The almost-parents are light, not bodies, no costumes clear. Ron and Hermione are present and respectfully out of focus.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the fire, young and old at once, grief he is only learning the shape of. His mouth is still. Ron looks at him and does not poke. Hermione looks at the book, giving him the silence.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C12-P3-P4

- **Beat:** The Stone is here, and someone inside the school wants it.
- **Image:** Hermione taps the table once, the detective again. A simple map of the castle is a folded letter she draws on: a dog, a trapdoor, an arrow inward. The danger is inside, not at the gates.
- **Setting:** The table. Night. The common room thinning out as students go to bed.
- **Who:** Hermione drawing with a quill, precise. Harry and Ron flanking, watching the arrow point at their own school. The ink dog is silly and serious.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the arrow, certain. Harry looks at it, the Halloween blood on Snape’s leg returning to his mind. Ron looks at the ink dog, grimacing, he remembers the teeth.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "The Stone is inside Hogwarts. And somebody in this castle wants it."
#### C12-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape. The bleeding leg. The third-floor corridor.
- **Image:** Harry counts it on his fingers, a child’s case. On the last finger he looks very sure. The fire pops like agreement.
- **Setting:** Late common room. Just the three. Embers.
- **Who:** Harry, three fingers up, glasses, scar, robes loosened. Ron nodding hard. Hermione listening, not fully sold, quill still.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, convinced, a little angry. Ron looks at Harry, all in. Hermione looks at the third finger, thoughtful, she wants one more proof and knows she may not get it.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Snape. His leg was bleeding the night of the troll. He had been up on the third floor, with the dog."
#### C12-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Ron agrees at once. Hermione says they need proof.
- **Image:** Ron’s hand slaps the table, yes. Hermione’s hand settles on the book, wait. Harry is between them, the theory already his. The panel holds the friendship and the disagreement without a fight.
- **Setting:** The dying fire. Bedtime. The portrait hole in the background, the Fat Lady dozing.
- **Who:** The three. Ron, eager fist. Hermione, book, caution. Harry, the scar and the certainty.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Hermione, impatient loyalty. Hermione looks at Harry, not at Ron, careful love of the truth. Harry looks at the embers, he will watch Snape. He is not angry at her. He is decided.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Then it's Snape." **Hermione:** "We need proof, Ron. Not a guess we like."

#### C12-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Quirrell passes the other way and flinches when he sees Snape.

- **Setting:** The corridor. Students giving both teachers room. Daylight.
- **Who:** Quirrell, purple robes, large turban, pale, clutching a book to his chest. Snape, black, tall, unbothered, papers. Harry peeking, seeing the flinch and filing it under Snape-is-scary, not under Quirrell-is-guilty.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Snape, fear, a sweat, eyes darting. Snape looks through him, cold. Harry looks at Quirrell’s fear and feels it proves his theory. He looks satisfied, which is the mistake.
- **Dialogue:** **Quirrell:** "S-sorry, Professor Snape. I was just p-passing."
#### C12-P4-P4

- **Image:** Two eyelines. Hermione watches Quirrell’s back, thoughtful, a question. Harry watches Snape’s back, case closed. They are looking opposite ways in the same panel.
- **Setting:** Behind the armor, or just stepping out. Afternoon. The corridor emptying.
- **Who:** Hermione, books, hair, head turned toward the purple turban. Harry, glasses, head turned toward the black robes. Ron between them, looking at both, not knowing who to follow.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Quirrell, uneasy, not ready to accuse, a small frown. Harry looks at Snape, certain, jaw set. Ron looks from one to the other, loyal to Harry, a little lost.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Did you see Professor Quirrell flinch?" **Harry:** "Everyone flinches at Snape."
#### C12-P4-P5

- **Beat:** That night Harry writes Flamel on a scrap and burns it.
- **Image:** In the dormitory, after lights out. Harry kneels by the stove or a candle. He writes the name, looks at it, and puts the scrap in the flame. Ash curls. He is afraid of the name on paper in a school where the thief lives.
- **Setting:** The Gryffindor boys’ dormitory. Four-posters, moonlight, Ron a lump in the next bed, Neville asleep, Trevor on a bedpost. A single candle.
- **Who:** Harry in gray pajamas, glasses, scar, kneeling, the scrap in his fingers, almost burned. Hedwig on a perch by the window, gold eyes open, watching him. Ron’s red hair on a pillow.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the burning name, serious, protective of the secret. Hedwig looks at him, calm. Ron does not look; he is asleep, mouth open, trusting Harry to still be there in the morning.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C12-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The ash curls. He says they have to keep the Stone where it is.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The ash lifts. Harry’s face is lit from below by the last of the flame. He is not speaking loudly; Ron is asleep. The promise is to himself and to the dark. Hedwig’s eyes are the witness.
- **Setting:** The dormitory, moonlight and a dying candle. The castle quiet. The third floor somewhere above, unshown, the dog standing on the Stone.
- **Who:** Harry, pajamas, glasses, empty fingers, ash on one thumb. Hedwig, white, gold-eyed. The sleeping friends as soft shapes.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ash, then toward the window and the castle, resolved. Young, stubborn, a little afraid. Hedwig looks at him, steady. The decision sits on his face more clearly than any word.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "We keep the Philosopher's Stone where it is. Whatever that takes."

---

## Chapter 14 — The Mirror of Erised

An empty castle, a father’s cloak, a mirror, and a warning.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 1 — The castle empties

#### W14-01-P1

- **Beat:** Hogwarts in the first snow, carriages at the door.
- **Image:** The castle exterior, pale winter sun, first snow, horseless carriages in a line at the steps. Students load trunks. Owls. The term is leaving.
- **Setting:** The entrance, a December morning.
- **Who:** Students and carriages. No close Harry yet.
- **Faces:** The crowd is cheerful. One window high up is not.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

- **Beat:** The doors, a river of scarves going out.
- **Image:** Four house scarves in the outflow, trunks, a mother already hugging someone at the bottom step. Harry is not in the river. He is inside, above.
- **Setting:** The front steps. Snow.
- **Who:** The crowd. Hedwig might be a white shape at a high window, small.
- **Faces:** Everyone else has somewhere to go.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

- **Beat:** The entrance hall, suddenly too big.
- **Image:** Inside, the hall is half empty, wet footprints, a last trunk. The noise is leaving. A staircase goes up toward the quiet.
- **Setting:** The entrance hall, morning, start of the holidays.
- **Who:** A few lingering students. Filch with a mop, grim, background.
- **Faces:** The room feels larger when it is abandoned.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

- **Image:** Gryffindor common room, almost empty, tree not the point yet or a small tree undecorated. Harry stands at the high window with Hedwig on his shoulder, watching the carriages. This is the interior before the old closer panel.
- **Setting:** The common room, winter morning.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes or a plain jumper, not the green Christmas jumper yet. That arrives on Christmas morning, later. Hedwig, snowy owl.
- **Faces:** He looks left. Hedwig looks out, gold eyes.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

- **Image:** Ron's trunk is open and then half unpacked again, maroon jumper, a letter in his hand from his mother. He looks at Harry's back and has not said he is staying. That line is the next old panel.
- **Setting:** The common room.
- **Who:** Ron, the letter, the trunk. Harry at the window.
- **Faces:** Ron looks awkward and kind. Harry has not turned.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
