- **Image:** Through the glass, carriages shrink into the snow. Harry's hand is up and not quite a wave. The old panel is this watch, closer. Here we see the room around him, empty chairs, and Ron about to speak.
- **Setting:** The common room, the second before Ron says he is staying.
- **Who:** Harry, Hedwig, Ron, the window.
- **Faces:** Harry looks as if staying is a fact, not a choice. Ron looks as if he is about to make it less lonely.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

### Page 2 — Staying

#### C13-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The castle empties. Carriages leave. Harry watches from a window.
- **Image:** From a high window, horseless carriages and students stream away across the snow-dusted lawn. The castle behind Harry is already quieter. His hand is on the cold glass.
- **Setting:** A corridor window, December, pale sun, first snow, the lake iron-gray. Trunks, scarves, goodbyes far below.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes, a red-and-gold scarf, glasses, scar, one palm on the window. He is staying. Below, tiny students, and one red-haired family group that will not all leave. Hedwig on his shoulder or in the reflection, white against the winter.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down, lonely and pretending not to be. His mouth is still. Hedwig looks out too, calm. He does not wave. No one is looking up for him.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C13-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Ron stays too, so Harry will not be alone.
- **Image:** Ron appears at Harry’s shoulder with a letter in Molly’s handwriting and a lopsided grin that means he argued with his mother and won a Christmas at school. Two scarves. Two boys. The carriages can go.
- **Setting:** The same window. Snow. A nearly empty corridor. A house-elf-free ordinary stone stillness.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, Weasley scarf, freckles, red hair, the letter half-folded, a packed night-bag at his feet that says he is staying. Harry, scarf, glasses, the loneliness interrupted.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, shy about the kindness, covering it with a joke in his eyes. Harry looks at him, surprised, then a real smile, the hollow filling. They look at the window together, and the leaving students matter less.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Mum wrote. She said I could come home, or stay so you wouldn't be on your own at Hogwarts. I'm staying."
#### C13-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Christmas morning. A pile of Weasley jumpers, one labeled for Harry.
- **Image:** The common room, morning, a small tree someone enchanted, real presents for once. A lumpy parcel in brown paper with Harry’s name in a mother’s hand. Ron is already wearing a new maroon sweater and looks both pleased and persecuted.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, Christmas morning, snow light, fire, almost empty, which makes the presents louder. Paper, string, a few cards.
- **Who:** Harry in pajamas and the school scarf, kneeling, the parcel in his lap. Ron in a fresh maroon hand-knit sweater, hair wild from sleep, his own opened boxes around him. Fred and George’s parcels are here too, identical paper, chaos pending.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his name on the paper, stunned, gentle. He looks at Ron. Ron looks at the sweater on his own body, groaning, and at Harry’s face, glad. A shy pride.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "That's your name on it. Mum knitted you one. She knits for everyone she decides is hers."
#### C13-P1-P4

- **Beat:** He pulls the homemade sweater on. The first gift that is only his.
- **Image:** The sweater goes over his head. It is too big, emerald green — Molly did not know his size and guessed with love — with a big H. His hair comes out worse. He looks down at the H and touches it.
- **Setting:** The common room. Paper around his knees. Snow light.
- **Who:** Harry, the green knit sweater, pajamas underneath, glasses back on, scar, both hands on the letter H. Ron in maroon, watching. No Dursley gift in sight. That absence is the background.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down at the sweater, a tight throat, a smile he does not aim at anyone. Ron looks at him, soft, a little embarrassed by his mother’s reach, and glad. Harry does not look at Ron yet. He is having a private first.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's mine. Just mine."
#### C13-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Fred and George set off crackers. Smoke and a terrible smell.
- **Image:** The twins, who stayed or whose gifts explode anyway — they are here, they stayed for the joke — yank a wizard cracker. It detonates into purple smoke and a smell that bends the tree. A hat or a bad joke falls out. Harry laughs.
- **Setting:** The common room, now purple. The tree tilted. Snow outside untouched and clean, a contrast.
- **Who:** Fred and George, identical red hair, matching sweaters with letters, identical wicked grins, the cracker between them. Harry in the green H sweater, laughing. Ron coughing, also laughing. A singed card.
- **Faces:** The twins look at each other, delighted with the damage. Harry looks at them, laughing with his whole face, the cupboard years not in this panel. Ron looks at the smoke, choking, happy.
- **Dialogue:** **Fred:** "Wizard cracker. Pull, George." **George:** "If it smells like a dungbomb, it worked."
#### C13-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry laughs with his whole face. Ron looks pleased with himself.
- **Image:** Page of warmth. Close on the two of them in the clearing smoke, sweaters, Christmas, no fame. Ron’s look says this was the plan: not the crackers, the staying.
- **Setting:** The common room settling. Purple haze. Fire. Snow light. The tree crooked and fine.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, glasses, messy hair, a paper crown from the cracker crooked on his head. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, a matching crown, arms folded, pretending he is not proud.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, bright, thank you without a speech. Ron looks at Harry, pleased, ears red, a grin he tries to make smaller. It does not get smaller.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "There. That's a proper Christmas."

### Page 3 — The cloak

#### C13-P2-P1

- **Beat:** One parcel has no name. The note says the cloak was his father’s.
- **Image:** Under the tree, a last parcel, soft, no tag. Harry holds a small note. His face goes still. The sweater morning becomes something older.
- **Setting:** The common room, later, twins gone to make trouble, Ron nearby. The fire low. The parcel is wrapped in plain paper, slightly silvery at the fold.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, glasses, scar, the note in both hands. Ron leaning in, curious, not grabbing. The parcel on Harry’s knees, unopened a second more.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the note, the smile gone, replaced by a careful ache. He looks at Ron, needing a witness. Ron looks at the note, then at Harry, quiet, the jokes parked. Respect.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "No name on the parcel. The note says the cloak was my father's. He left it in Dumbledore's keeping."
#### C13-P2-P2

- **Beat:** He lifts the cloth. His hand reads as gone. The rest of him does not.
- **Image:** The cloak spills out, fluid, silver-gray, more water than wool, a sheer silver-gray shimmer at the edge. Harry’s hand goes under it. Ron thinks the hand has gone, and the fire shows through the cloth, but the hand is still a faint figure: 90% transparent, only about 10% opacity, faint but clearly Harry’s hand, fingers still readable. Not a deleted wrist. Ron’s eyes are huge.
- **Setting:** Close on their knees and the cloth. Firelight. No one else. The common room does not change.
- **Who:** Harry, green H jumper, the cloak in his lap. The covered hand is a faint 10%-opacity hand, not an empty cuff. His face, hair, glasses, and scar stay solid and clear because they are not under the cloth yet. Ron, both hands hovering, not touching yet, red hair, freckles. The note on the floor.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the faint hand, wonder and a lump in the throat. He looks like a boy meeting his father through an object. Ron looks at the shimmer, delighted and hushed, and he can still see a ghost of the fingers. He looks at Harry’s face and does not cheer loudly.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Your hand. Harry, your hand has gone."
#### C13-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Night. His shoes show under the hem. The rest of him is a faint figure, not deleted.
- **Image:** A corridor at night. Harry’s sneakers are the solid part under the hem. Above them, Harry is 90% transparent, only about 10% opacity: a faint but clearly Harry shape, face, messy hair, scar side, glasses, green H jumper and gray pajamas still distinguishable. The cloak edge is a sheer silver-gray shimmer. Readers must see him. Not a pair of shoes with nobody above them.
- **Setting:** A sleeping corridor. Suits of armor. A window of snow. Torches low. Christmas night, the castle hollow.
- **Who:** Harry under the cloak, faint 10%-opacity figure, shoes solid, green jumper and pajamas readable through the cloth, glasses and scar side still findable. No Ron in this panel; he is lookout or asleep. Harry wanted the first walk alone.
- **Faces:** The face is faint and still Harry’s: cautious, free, a little lonely. A portrait’s eyes follow him. She can almost see a boy, and she is puzzled, not looking at empty air.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C13-P2-P4

- **Beat:** He slips past Filch. Filch sniffs and walks on.
- **Image:** Filch and Mrs. Norris stand in a pool of lamp light. Harry passes behind them, close enough to touch the cat’s tail. His shoes are solid. The rest of him is 90% transparent, only about 10% opacity, a faint but clearly Harry figure in the green jumper, face and scar side still readable, cloak edge a sheer silver-gray shimmer. The cat’s head turns. Filch sniffs, suspicious, and walks on. He does not see the boy the reader can see.
- **Setting:** A night corridor, Filch’s lamp, Mrs. Norris’s eye-shine. Christmas stillness.
- **Who:** Filch, shabby coat, lamp, keys, stringy hair, nose lifted. Mrs. Norris, tail twitching, looking at the faint figure. Harry, shoes solid, the rest a 10%-opacity boy just leaving the lamp, not a dust puff and not an empty pair of shoes.
- **Faces:** Filch looks at the dark, suspicious, then dismisses it, sour. He does not focus on the faint face. Mrs. Norris looks at Harry and knows. Harry’s faint face is held breath, eyes on Filch’s back.
- **Dialogue:** **Filch:** "I smell students. Mrs Norris, find them."
#### C13-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A door he has never seen.
- **Image:** The shoes stop. A door stands where a wall was, or a door he passed all term and never opened. It is tall, unused, a little silver in the carving. His hand depresses the handle. The hand is faint, 90% transparent, only about 10% opacity, still clearly Harry’s hand, and the rest of him is the same faint figure beside the door. Not a handle moving in an empty frame.
- **Setting:** A forgotten bit of castle, dust, a tapestry of a wizard chess game, moonlight, no torches. Far from the common room. Sheer silver-gray cloak edge.
- **Who:** Harry, faint but readable: face, messy hair, scar side, glasses, green jumper, gray pajamas, solid shoes. The cloak over him. The door.
- **Faces:** His faint face looks at the door, cautious, hoping. The door looks like it has been waiting. A reader can see who is opening it.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C13-P2-P6

- **Beat:** A dark room. Something tall under a sheet.
- **Image:** He steps in and the cloak slips back so we see him again, small, in the green sweater and pajamas, glasses, the cloak over one arm. In the center of a bare room, a tall shape under a dusty white sheet. Moon from a high window. His breath clouds.
- **Setting:** An unused classroom or chamber. Desks pushed aside. Dust. One window. No fire. The sheeted object is the only furniture that matters, man-high and a little wider.
- **Who:** Harry, visible now, green H sweater, gray pajamas, slippers, glasses, scar, the invisibility cloak bundled in his arms. He holds it against his chest like proof his father was real. The sheeted mirror, unknown.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the sheet, curious, cautious, the Christmas laugh far away. He looks young. He takes a step. His face is open, unguarded, which is dangerous in this room.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 4 — The parents

#### C13-P3-P1

- **Beat:** He pulls the sheet. An ornate mirror. Only himself.
- **Image:** The sheet slithers off. An old, tall mirror with a carved frame, words along the top too worn to read easily. In it: Harry, sweater, cloak in his arms, and the empty room. Ordinary. He is almost disappointed.
- **Setting:** The bare chamber, moonlight, dust in the air from the sheet. The mirror dominates.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, pajamas, glasses, scar, cloak bundled. His reflection, the same, accurate, alone. No one else in the glass. Yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his reflection, a small frown, the expectation deflating. The reflection looks back, same expression. Lonely and ordinary. He was hoping without admitting it.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Just me. That's all it shows."
#### C13-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Then figures appear behind the reflection.
- **Image:** Over the reflection’s shoulders, the glass clouds and clears into people. They are not in the room. They are only in the mirror. Harry’s real shoulders, in front of the glass, have no one behind them. The panel shows both truths: empty room, crowded glass.
- **Setting:** The mirror and the moonlit room, split by the glass.
- **Who:** Real Harry, alone in the room, green H jumper, cloak lowered, back mostly toward us, centered, looking into the glass. In the glass, the same arrangement every Mirror panel: Lily on the viewer’s left of his reflection, long straight dark-red hair, green eyes, the same soft pale-green dress. James on the viewer’s right, late twenties, messy black hair, round glasses, no beard, no scar, the same simple dark jacket over a light shirt. They stand close behind him, hands near his shoulders, James a little taller. Softer unnamed faces may fade behind those two. They do not swap sides.
- **Faces:** Real Harry looks at the glass, frozen. Mirror-Harry looks astonished. Lily, on the viewer’s left, looks at him with love. James, on the viewer’s right, looks at him with pride and a gentle sadness. They look at Harry, not at each other.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Mum? Dad?"
#### C13-P3-P3

- **Beat:** A woman with kind eyes. A man with glasses like his.
- **Image:** Close inside the mirror. Same sides as every other panel. Lily, on the viewer’s left, long straight dark-red hair, green eyes, the same pale-green dress, a soft smile, her hand lifting. James, on the viewer’s right, round glasses, messy black hair, no beard, the same dark jacket, a warm grin Harry has never seen on his own face. They do not trade places in the close-up.
- **Setting:** Only the glass. A warm light that does not match the moon. No room. Harry’s reflection is still center, shorter than both.
- **Who:** Lily on the viewer’s left, pale-green dress, one hand raised. James on the viewer’s right, dark jacket over a light shirt, a hand on mirror-Harry’s shoulder that the real Harry cannot feel. Mirror-Harry between them, small, green sweater, back mostly toward us. No robes. No swapped sides.
- **Faces:** Lily looks at Harry, tender, knowing him. James looks at Harry, grinning, proud, the grin a little broken by the glass. Harry looks from the woman on the viewer’s left to the man on the viewer’s right, starving.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C13-P3-P4

- **Beat:** He turns. The room is empty.
- **Image:** He spins. Moonlight, dust, desks, the sheet on the floor. No one. His hand is out, reaching for a shoulder that is not there. The mirror is behind him, still glowing faintly on his back.
- **Setting:** The bare chamber. Cold. Empty. The contrast is the point.
- **Who:** Harry alone, green sweater, pajamas, glasses, scar, one hand reaching into nothing, the cloak dropped at his feet. No parents. The mirror’s frame at the edge, behind him.
- **Faces:** He looks at the empty air, wrecked, a child’s betrayal by physics. Eyes wet. Mouth open. He looks smaller than in the glass.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Where did you go?"
#### C13-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He turns back. They wave. His hand on the glass.
- **Image:** He is pressed to the mirror. Both palms flat. His forehead nearly touches it, scar against the cool surface. Inside, his parents press their hands to the same place. They do not quite meet. The glass is a fact.
- **Setting:** The mirror, moonlight, his breath fogging the lower corner and fading.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, pajamas, hands spread on the glass, glasses, tears he is not wiping, back mostly toward us, centered. In the glass, Lily is still on the viewer’s left, pale-green dress, long straight dark-red hair, green eyes, her hand opposite his. James is still on the viewer’s right, dark jacket, messy black hair, round glasses, no beard, his hand opposite the other palm. Same height, close behind his reflection. The family crowd stays soft behind them. They have not swapped.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his mother on the viewer’s left, then his father on the viewer’s right, greedy and gentle, crying without sound. Lily looks at him, loving, she cannot come through. James looks at him, the grin softened to something that hurts. They look only at him.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Stay. Please stay."
#### C13-P3-P6

- **Beat:** He sits on the floor until the fire dies. There is no fire. He sits until the moon moves.
- **Image:** Time passes in one picture: the moon has traveled in the window. Harry sits on the dusty floor, knees up, still facing the glass, the cloak around his shoulders now. His parents are still there whenever he looks. He looks thin and willing to stay forever.
- **Setting:** The chamber, colder, moon moved, dust, the sheet forgotten. Night deep.
- **Who:** Harry, solid and fully visible, the cloak only around his shoulders, not hiding him, green sweater, pajamas, slippers, glasses, sitting center, back mostly toward us. In the glass the same pair, still not swapped: Lily on the viewer’s left in the pale-green dress, James on the viewer’s right in the dark jacket, hands near the reflection’s shoulders, waving, patient, which is part of the danger. No one comes to get him. Yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, exhausted, happy, lost. A smile that is not healthy. Lily, viewer’s left, looks at him, and if a mirror could worry, her green eyes would. He does not see the worry. He sees only that she is there. James, viewer’s right, stays the same warm unshaven face.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 5 — The warning

#### C13-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Another night. Ron sees himself with a captain’s badge and a cup. Harry cannot see them.
- **Image:** Ron stands where Harry stood, cloak off, and laughs. This panel is Ron’s wish only. In the glass he is older, a Quidditch captain’s badge, a cup in his hands, taller, a crowd that is only his. Lily and James are not in this glass. Harry stands beside the real Ron and cannot see the cup.
- **Setting:** The same chamber, another night. The cloak in Harry’s arms. Moon. One mirror, showing Ron’s desire, not the Potters.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, red hair, in front of the glass, delighted. Mirror-Ron, older, badge, cup. Harry beside him, green sweater, seeing none of that triumph. No Lily. No James. No pale-green dress and no dark jacket in this frame.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his glorious self, a huge grin, ears red, wanting it and joking because he wants it. Harry looks at the glass, confused, he cannot see the cup. He looks at Ron’s real face, realizing the mirror is not a window. It is a wish.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "I'm Quidditch captain. I've got the House Cup. I'm Head Boy as well. Harry, look." **Harry:** "I can't see any of that."
#### C13-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Ron cannot see the Potters. The mirror is different for each of them.
- **Image:** A clean split panel. One half is Ron’s glass only: himself triumphant, badge, cup. No parents in that half. The other half is Harry’s glass: he stands center, back mostly toward us, Lily on the viewer’s left in the pale-green dress, James on the viewer’s right in the dark jacket. They do not cross into Ron’s half.
- **Setting:** The mirror room, moonlight, dust. Same mirror, two wishes, not one blended family.
- **Who:** Both boys, sweaters, pajamas. Ron’s half: older Ron, captain’s badge, cup. Harry’s half: Lily, long straight dark-red hair, green eyes, pale-green dress, viewer’s left; James, messy black hair, round glasses, no beard, dark jacket, viewer’s right; hands near Harry’s shoulders. Same clothes as the night before.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, the grin fading into curiosity, a little guilt that his wish was a trophy. Harry looks at Ron, the privacy of his parents suddenly obvious. They look at each other with more care. Neither mocks what the other wanted. Lily and James look at Harry only, and only inside his half.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I see my parents. You can't see them, can you." **Ron:** "The mirror's telling a different lie to each of us."
#### C13-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Dumbledore is suddenly there, as if he stepped out of the dark.
- **Image:** They turn. Dumbledore is in the room, tall hat brushing dust, no cloak-rustle, no door open. He looks at the mirror, not at them first, as if greeting an old acquaintance he does not trust.
- **Setting:** The chamber. He brought no lamp. The moon and the mirror-light are enough. The door is still shut.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple robes with stars, half-moon spectacles, long silver beard, pointed hat, hands folded. Harry, cloak in his arms, guilty. Ron, a step behind Harry, startled. The mirror behind them, now showing something we do not fully see for Dumbledore, perhaps only his own eyes, carefully not revealed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the mirror, familiar and sad. Then he looks at Harry, kind, not angry. Harry looks up, caught, the child who stayed too long. Ron looks at Dumbledore, awed into silence.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Good evening, Harry. Mr Weasley. I see you have found the Mirror of Erised."
#### C13-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The mirror shows the deepest desire of the heart, and not the truth.
- **Image:** Dumbledore speaks gently, one hand indicating the glass without touching it. In the glass, faintly, Harry’s parents fade because he is listening to a living person. If they are still readable, Lily is on the viewer’s left in the pale-green dress and James is on the viewer’s right in the dark jacket. They do not swap while they fade. The lesson is the picture: the wish thins when he looks away.
- **Setting:** The three of them and the mirror. Moon. Quiet.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, tall, beard, spectacles, a hand open toward the frame. Harry looking at him, cloak clutched, green sweater, back no longer to the glass. Ron listening, the captain fantasy not mocked. In the glass, the same parents, fading, sides unchanged. Not Ron’s cup.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, tender and firm. Harry looks at him, taking it in, still wanting to look back at the glass. Ron looks at the floor, understanding his cup was a wish too. No one looks triumphant.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "The Mirror of Erised shows the deepest wish of the heart. Not the truth. Erised is desire, written backwards, if you enjoy a riddle."
#### C13-P4-P5

- **Beat:** People have wasted away in front of it. It will be moved.
- **Image:** Dumbledore’s face, close, the kindness edged with a warning. Behind him, a suggestion of other people, not shown clearly, pale, who sat too long — only empty chairs in the dust, a visual metaphor, not ghosts of strangers. The sheet is in his other hand. He means to cover it.
- **Setting:** The chamber. He is between Harry and the glass.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple, the sheet gathered. Harry, small, the cloak, looking past Dumbledore’s arm for one more glimpse. Ron at the door, ready to leave, looking back for Harry.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, loving, the warning real. Harry looks at the sliver of mirror, aching, obedient anyway. Ron looks at Harry, a friend’s come-on, gentle.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Wizards have sat down in front of it and wasted away. The mirror will be moved. Do not come looking."
#### C13-P4-P6

- **Beat:** A dream is a poor place to live. Harry looks back once.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They are at the door. Dumbledore remains by the mirror. Harry looks back once over the green sweater and his father’s cloak. The mirror shows his parents one last time, small in the distance, waving. Even small, Lily is on the viewer’s left and James is on the viewer’s right, same pale-green dress, same dark jacket. He does not go back. His face is grief and a choice.
- **Setting:** The doorway. Moon. The corridor ahead, which is the castle, which is living. Snow light. Ron already in the hall.
- **Who:** Harry in the doorway, cloak over his arm, green Christmas sweater, glasses, scar, one hand on the frame. Ron in the corridor, waiting, not rushing him. Dumbledore inside, tall, watching Harry choose the corridor. In the glass, tiny and still not swapped: Lily viewer’s left, James viewer’s right, hands near the small reflection’s shoulders.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back, eyes wet, a goodbye. Then he looks at Ron, choosing the friend who is actually here. Dumbledore looks at Harry, proud and sad. Ron looks at Harry, patient, there. The parents look at their son, and the door closes.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "A dream is a poor place to live, Harry. Stay out here, with the people who can answer when you speak." **Harry:** "Yes, Professor."
---

## Chapter 15 — Norbert the Norwegian Ridgeback

An illegal egg, a bite, a spy, a midnight handoff, and detention.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Setting:** The grounds at dusk, before the egg is shown.
- **Who:** The three in cloaks over robes.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks suspicious of surprises. Ron looks hungry. Harry looks fond.

### Page 2 — The egg

#### C14-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid’s hut. He is grinning too widely, hiding something.
- **Image:** The door opens on Hagrid filling it, smile too big for an ordinary visit. One hand is behind his back. Fang’s tail thumps. The boys and Hermione are on the step with a teapot excuse, and they can already tell.
- **Setting:** The hut at dusk. The forest edge dark. A warm interior, fire, the crossbow, herbs. Spring-ish light after Christmas, the grounds muddy and green again.
- **Who:** Hagrid, moleskin coat half off, huge knit vest, beard, the hidden hand. Harry, school robes, glasses, scarf loose. Ron, maroon sweater. Hermione, books, already suspicious. Fang, black boarhound, delighted, drooling.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them, bursting, trying to look casual and failing. Harry looks at the hidden hand, a smile starting. Hermione looks at Hagrid’s eyes, wary. Ron looks at Fang, then at Hagrid, curious. Fang looks at everyone, pure dog.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Come in, come in. Don't mind Fang. I've a surprise."
#### C14-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A black egg in the fire. He says he won it at cards.
- **Image:** The fire is banked high. In the coals sits a large black egg, leathery, bigger than a pumpkin, rocking slightly from the heat. Hagrid’s hands are spread over it like a proud parent. A deck of cards is on the table, a losing hand still laid out.
- **Setting:** The hearth. Sparks. The hut crowded with the children now inside. Night settling outside the window.
- **Who:** Hagrid kneeling, vest, beard almost in the fire, coat sleeves rolled. The egg, black, scaled, hot. Harry, Ron, and Hermione crowded behind him, faces lit orange. Fang kept back by a chair, whimpering.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the egg, besotted. Harry looks at the egg, uneasy wonder. Ron looks at it, a grin he knows he should not have. Hermione looks at the cards, then the egg, the legal problem already on her face.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Won it off a stranger, cards, down the pub. A dragon egg. Norwegian Ridgeback, if I'm any judge."
#### C14-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says dragon breeding is illegal. Hagrid says it is only an egg.
- **Image:** Hermione with both palms up, the rule offered plainly. Hagrid lifts the egg a little, as if its being unhatched is a defense. Ash on his beard. She is not persuaded. He is not listening.
- **Setting:** The hearth, tight. The egg between them like a third person.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, no book needed, she knows this one. Hagrid, huge, the egg in a folded blanket now, tender. Harry and Ron in the middle, literally, looking from one to the other.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Hagrid, firm, worried for him more than for the rule. Hagrid looks at the egg, not at her, defensive and dreamy. Harry looks at Hermione, agreeing, and at Hagrid, not wanting to crush him. Ron looks at the egg, the grin losing to sense.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Dragon breeding is against wizard law, Hagrid. The Ministry would send you to Azkaban." **Hagrid:** "It's only an egg. An egg isn't a dragon yet."
#### C14-P1-P4

- **Beat:** The egg rocks. A crack.
- **Image:** A crack runs. A chip falls into the coals. All four of them lean in so fast their heads nearly knock. The sound is small and enormous.
- **Setting:** The fire, night, the hut gone quiet except the crack. Fang’s ears up.
- **Who:** The egg, a bright line in the black shell. Hagrid’s huge fingers hovering, not touching, afraid to help wrong. Three children’s faces in a row, orange-lit: glasses, freckles, bushy hair.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the crack, breathless, joy. Hermione looks at it, dread and awe fighting. Harry looks at it, the wonder winning for one second. Ron looks at it, mouth open.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "It's cracking."
#### C14-P1-P5

- **Beat:** A wet, ugly, beautiful head. Hagrid names him Norbert.
- **Image:** A dragon’s head pushes out, small, slick, spines crumpled, eyes milky then clearing, a thread of smoke from the nostrils. It is ugly. It is beautiful. Hagrid is crying. He whispers the name.
- **Setting:** The hearth. Shell pieces. Firelight making the wet scales shine. The hut a nursery it should not be.
- **Who:** Norbert, a baby Norwegian Ridgeback, black-bronze, tiny wings glued down, claws already too sharp, head the size of a cat and growing in the minute. Hagrid’s hands a cradle. Harry, Ron, Hermione a step back, heat on their faces.
- **Faces:** Norbert looks at Hagrid, imprinting, a squeak. Hagrid looks at him, wrecked with love. Harry looks at the dragon, soft despite himself. Hermione looks at Hagrid, the scolding paused, she sees the love and it complicates her. Ron looks at the claws, delighted and doomed.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Hello, Norbert. Who's a lovely little Ridgeback, then?"
#### C14-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Over the flames, the three friends look at each other. This is a disaster.
- **Image:** The dragon and Hagrid are a warm blur. In the sharp foreground, three faces turn toward each other. No one has to say it. The fire pops. A decision will be required and none of them knows it yet.
- **Setting:** The hut, night, forest pressing the window.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, robe collar open from the heat. Ron, freckles, a scorch mark on his sleeve from leaning in. Hermione, hair frizzed by the fire, mouth a line. Behind, Hagrid rocking a dragon.
- **Faces:** They look at one another, the same thought. Harry looks worried. Hermione looks resolute. Ron looks like he wants to laugh and hide. Friendship as a silent vote: this cannot stay.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "This is a disaster." **Harry:** "We can't leave him in the hut."

Harry under the slipping cloak is still a faint but clear boy, 90% transparent, only about 10% opacity, face and scar readable, one shoe solid where the cloth has slipped. Hermione the same faint readable figure, furious. Ron pale, bandage, the crate. Norbert’s tail. Not three blank shapes.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, victorious, the courtyard mockery upgraded to a real crime. Harry looks at him, cold, not begging. Hermione looks at Draco, contempt. Ron looks at the tail, then at Draco, a spark of we-still-have-to-finish-this.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "A dragon. I'm telling Professor McGonagall, Potter. Enjoy being expelled."
#### C14-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They lose him on a moving staircase.
- **Image:** One entire flight turns as a single solid object. Every step stays fixed to the other steps. Both railings are part of that same flight and turn with it. The steps do not walk, shuffle, or detach. The lower end stays on its landing and is the hinge. The upper end swings away from the landing it used to meet, and a gap opens between that moving upper end and the landing it is leaving. Draco is on the flight, almost losing balance: feet planted on the steps, body jerked sideways, one hand grabbing the railing that is turning with him, the other hand still reaching, robes flaring, shout swallowed. He is not calmly walking. Harry, Hermione, and Ron are on the landing they needed, stopped, staring at the turning flight, the smoking crate with them. Nobody else is on the flight. For a second the castle itself takes their side.
- **Setting:** A Hogwarts stairwell, night, moonlight through tall windows, stone. Camera, the same angle as every panel where the stairs are actually turning: three-quarter view from a landing, low enough that the bottom hinge and the top end are both visible mid-swing, with the gap opening between the moving upper end and the landing it is leaving. Not a flat head-on view. The bottom hinge is in frame, not cropped out. Only this one flight is turning. The other flights are still.
- **Who:** Draco, on the moving flight, platinum hair, fury, feet planted, body jerked sideways, one hand locked on the turning railing, the other still reaching. Harry, Hermione, and Ron on the landing, not on the flight, cloak askew, faces turned toward the flight, the smoking crate beside them. Norbert scrabbling inside. No second flight in motion.
- **Faces:** Draco looks back, the victory cracking into rage, almost falling. Harry looks at the swinging gap, a breath of thanks, not a smile, face turned toward the flight. Hermione stares at the turning flight, no time. Ron stares at the flight, dizzy, still on the landing.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "The staircase moved. You cheat, Potter!"

- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, late, fire, a crowd that was celebrating nothing and now has a reason to be angry. Red hangings. The Fat Lady’s portrait swinging shut, she heard.
- **Who:** Harry, Hermione, Ron in the entrance, detention written on their posture. Neville by the fire, sad for them, toad in his lap. Fred and George, unusually unsmiling. Other students, folded arms. Hermione’s hair still wild from the tower.

Fred looks at Ron, not cruel, just tired of losing points. Harry looks at no one, the cloak gone, the dragon gone, the cost staying. Neville looks at Harry, sympathetic, which helps and hurts.
- **Dialogue:** **Fred:** "Fifty points, was it? Each? Brilliant work, little brother."

- **Setting:** The boys’ dormitory, four-posters, moonlight, Neville asleep, Trevor a lump. Quiet after the common room’s stares.
- **Who:** Harry, gray pajamas, no sweater, glasses off on the nightstand so his face is more vulnerable, scar clear, messy hair on the pillow. Ron asleep in the next bed, bandage, red hair. Hedwig at the window, awake, gold eyes.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ceiling, hollow, guilty, missing his father in the form of a cloth. Hedwig looks at him, steady. Ron’s face is slack with fever-sleep, not available. The panel is bare on purpose.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

## Chapter 16 — The Forbidden Forest

Lanterns, a dead unicorn, a hood, a centaur, and the reason.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Who:** Harry, school cloak, glasses, scar, lantern in his hand because Draco would not take it. Draco, platinum hair limp from the damp, fine cloak muddied at the hem, empty hands, wand pocketed, too proud to draw it.

- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "If we die, Potter, I am blaming you." **Harry:** "Then walk, and don't die."

- **Who:** The two boys at the edge. The still shape.
- **Faces:** Harry looks sick. Draco makes no sound yet.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
#### W16-07-P6

- **Beat:** Harry's mouth opens. The word moonlight is next.
- **Image:** He is about to lie to himself out loud. Draco is about to believe him because he wants to. The hood is not here. The next old panel is the line.
- **Setting:** The clearing's edge, the second before Harry speaks.
- **Who:** Harry and Draco, the silver beyond.
- **Faces:** Harry looks hopeful and wrong. Draco looks ready to agree.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

### Page 4 — The blood

#### C15-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A silver shape. Harry thinks it is moonlight.
- **Image:** Ahead, between trunks, a pale glow on the ground. Harry lifts the lantern, hoping it is the moon through a gap. There is no gap. The light is coming from the thing itself.
- **Setting:** A clearing about to open. Fog. Black trees. The glow silver-white, liquid-looking.
- **Who:** Harry in front, lantern, glasses catching the silver. Draco a step behind, the sneer gone because he has not remembered to wear it. Their cloaks damp.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the glow, hopeful for a second, then uncertain. Draco looks at it, and the uncertainty hits him faster. He looks at Harry, not for friendship, for confirmation that this is bad.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's moonlight. That's all it is."
#### C15-P2-P2

- **Beat:** A dead unicorn. The moonlight is its blood.
- **Image:** The clearing. A unicorn lies on its side, white coat, slender, horn broken or dim, beautiful and wrong. Silver blood pools and crawls, luminous, staining the moss. It is not gory in a cheap way. It is sacred and ruined.
- **Setting:** A small clearing. Fog. The silver pool is the only light besides the lantern, and it is brighter. Flowers bent. No birds.
- **Who:** The unicorn, dead, slender legs folded wrong, mane in the blood. Harry and Draco at the edge, small, cloaks, the lantern useless against that shine. No attacker yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the unicorn, grief, a hand half-up as if he could still help. His mouth is open. Draco looks at it, the pure-blood boy who knows the weight of this, genuinely shaken. The sneer is gone. Fear is clean on him.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's a unicorn. It's dead. That shine is unicorn blood."
#### C15-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Draco makes a small sound and steps back.
- **Image:** Draco’s foot slips in moss. He steps back into a tree, one hand up, the sound small and childlike. Harry does not mock him. Harry is not looking at him. Harry is looking at the far side of the clearing, where the pool is disturbed.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. Silver light. A dark gap in the trees opposite.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, cloak, back to a trunk, pale. Harry, lantern lowered, glasses, scar beginning to prick, body still. The unicorn in the foreground, still.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the blood, then at the dark gap, a whimper he hates. Harry looks at the gap, focused, the scar a warning. He looks calm and he is not.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "I don't like this. Potter, I really don't like this."
#### C15-P2-P4

- **Beat:** A hooded figure crouched over the body, drinking.
- **Image:** The figure is there, as if it was always in the dark and the dark sat up. A cloak with a hood, no face, bent to the unicorn’s throat, silver blood at the hood’s edge, drinking. It is the most wrong thing in a forest of wrong things.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The silver pool. The hooded shape breaking it.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tattered dark cloak, hunched, hands like pale spiders, face hidden, mouth at the wound. The unicorn. Harry and Draco small at the edge, Harry’s lantern shaking a fraction.
- **Faces:** No face in the hood, only a blackness and a glint. Harry looks at it, horror, scar blazing under his hair. Draco looks at it, the last of his posture gone. The figure does not look at them yet. It is feeding.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C15-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Harry’s scar tears. He drops to a knee.
- **Image:** Pain is a white crack across the panel. Harry is down on one knee, hand clamped to his forehead, lantern fallen, its light rolling. The hood lifts because the pain called it, or because he made a sound.
- **Setting:** Moss, silver blood nearby, the lantern on its side. The clearing tilting.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak, glasses askew, both knees wanting to go, one hand on the scar, the other in the moss. Draco standing but barely, behind him. The hooded figure straightening.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are squeezed, pain, he looks at the ground not the figure. Draco looks at Harry, then at the hood, and the calculation is simple: run. The hood looks toward Harry. The glint in it finds him.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "My scar. Draco, my scar is splitting open."
#### C15-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The hood lifts. No readable face. Draco runs.
- **Image:** The hood comes up. What is under it is not a face the panel will explain: a blur, a paleness, a wrongness, Voldemort not restored. Draco breaks. He runs back down the path, cloak flying, platinum hair, abandoning Harry without a speech. Harry is still on one knee.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The path back. Silver light. The figure between Harry and the unicorn.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tall now, cloak, unreadable head, a step toward Harry. Draco running, back to us, fast, cowardice and survival the same motion. Harry kneeling, glasses, scar, lantern out of reach.
- **Faces:** Draco does not look back. His back is the expression: panic. Harry looks up at the hood, unable to stand, seeing nothing he can name. The hood looks at Harry, and the scar answers.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "I am not staying for that. I am not."

### Page 5 — Firenze

#### C15-P3-P1

- **Beat:** The figure glides toward Harry. He cannot stand.
- **Image:** The hooded thing does not walk so much as arrive closer. Harry’s legs will not obey. Silver blood on the figure’s mouth, or where a mouth would be. Harry’s hand finds his wand and it feels like a twig.
- **Setting:** The clearing, fog torn by movement. The unicorn behind the figure like an accusation.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, cloak dragging through the silver pool, pale hands out. Harry on the ground, school cloak, wand in a shaking hand, glasses, scar white-hot. No Draco. No lantern steady.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the hands, terror held down by pain. He looks very small. The hood looks at him with a hunger that is not only for blood. No readable features, so the hunger is in the lean of the body.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C15-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Hooves. A centaur blocks the hood.
- **Image:** Hooves crash into the clearing. A centaur rears between Harry and the hood, a living wall: human torso, horse body, bow up, hair flying. The hood recoils from him, not from Harry’s wand.
- **Setting:** The clearing, suddenly violent with motion. Moss torn. Silver splashed.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino body, cream-gold coat, bare chest, long blond hair, a bow, stern young face, hooves between Harry and death. The hooded figure shrinking back. Harry on the ground behind the centaur’s legs, looking up at a savior he did not expect.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the hood, cold, ancient anger, no fear. The hood looks at him, reluctant, hungry, retreating. Harry looks at Firenze’s back, shock and relief crashing together.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Back, foul thing. You will not drink and hunt in the same hour."
#### C15-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Firenze tells Harry to climb on. Harry does.
- **Image:** Firenze kneels, impossible grace, and Harry scrambles onto the horse back, clumsy, fists in the pale mane. The hood is at the tree line, not gone, watching.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. The unicorn still. The path away.
- **Who:** Firenze, kneeling, blond hair, a hand back to help, bow in the other. Harry, cloak tangled, glasses, swinging a leg up, small on the broad back. The hooded figure, paused between trees.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks toward the path, urgent, calm voice, he does not look afraid but he will not linger. Harry looks at the mane, then at the hood, he does not want to turn his back and he does. The hood looks at Harry being taken, a promise more than a glare.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Harry Potter. Climb on. Now."
#### C15-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They move. The hood does not follow past the centaur.
- **Image:** The forest streaks. Firenze gallops. Harry clings. Behind, at the edge of the silver clearing, the hood stands still, unwilling to cross some line the centaur carries with him. Then the trees take the view.
- **Setting:** The forest at speed. Trunks, fog, mane, the clearing shrinking.
- **Who:** Firenze running, powerful, hair and Harry’s cloak streaming together. Harry bent low, arms around him as much as pride allows, glasses pressed to his face. The hood small and still behind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back once, the scar easing with distance, fear still high. Firenze looks ahead, profile stern, he does not look back. The hood looks after them, patient. It can wait. It has been waiting.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C15-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Firenze says he has never seen a unicorn die like that.
- **Image:** They have stopped at a stream. Firenze’s head is bowed, the sternness cracked by grief. Harry slides down, legs unsteady. The forest is quiet in a mourning way.
- **Setting:** A moonlit stream, safer trees, water over stones, no silver blood. The castle is still far.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino, blond hair shadowing his face, bow lowered. Harry, cloak torn at the hem by branches, glasses, scar faded from white-hot to sore, standing small beside a centaur.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the water, grieved, angry. Harry looks at him, the question waiting in his mouth. He looks young and polite and badly shaken. He does not rush the centaur’s grief.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "I have never seen a unicorn slain like that. Their blood is a terrible thing to spill."
#### C15-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Harry, still holding the scar, asks what that thing was.
- **Image:** His fingers are on the scar again, a habit now. He looks up the long height of Firenze. The question is the panel. An owl. The stream.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Moon. A centaur and a boy.
- **Who:** Harry, school cloak, pajamas or clothes under it from detention, glasses, scar under his fingers. Firenze, bare chest, horse body, bow, looking down.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, needing the name, afraid of it. Firenze looks at Harry, measuring whether a child should hear this. His face is kind and severe. He decides to tell.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "What was under that hood? My scar knew it before I did."

### Page 6 — The curse

#### C15-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Unicorn blood will keep you alive an inch from death.
- **Image:** Firenze speaks. In the air between them, Harry imagines it without a full vision panel: the hood at the unicorn’s throat, the silver keeping a ruined thing in the world. Harry’s hand stays on his scar.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon on the water. Quiet.
- **Who:** Firenze, gesturing once, restrained. Harry listening, cloak wrapped tight, glasses, small.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, unwilling teacher. Harry looks at him, the horror settling into sense. He looks ill. He looks angry on the unicorn’s behalf.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Unicorn blood will hold you in the world, even an inch from death. That is why the hooded thing was drinking."
#### C15-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The price is a half-life, cursed from the moment you drink.
- **Image:** Firenze’s face, close, the sentence landing. Harry takes a step back without meaning to, not from Firenze, from the idea. The stream goes on, clean, a contrast with cursed blood.
- **Setting:** Moonlight, water, trees.
- **Who:** Firenze’s face, blond hair, solemn eyes. Harry, a step off, cloak, the scar.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, pity and warning. Harry looks at the ground, imagining a half-life, then back up, braver. He asks with his eyes: who would pay that.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "The price is a half-life. Cursed, from the first swallow. A monster's bargain."
#### C15-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Harry says the name he is afraid of. Firenze does not correct him.
- **Image:** Harry says it. The trees do not like it. Firenze’s ears — human — do not flinch into denial. A leaf falls. The name is accepted. That is the answer.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Stillness after a dangerous word.
- **Who:** Harry, mouth just closed on the name, glasses, cloak, fists. Firenze, towering, a short nod.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, hoping to be told he is wrong. Firenze looks at him, grave, he will not give that comfort. Harry’s face changes: the hope closes. Knowledge arrives.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It was Voldemort." **Firenze:** "I will not correct you."
#### C15-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort is in these trees because of the Stone.
- **Image:** Firenze turns his head toward the castle, far through the trees, a few lights. The line from the forest to the school is the story. Harry follows the look. The Stone is in there. The thing in the hood wants it.
- **Setting:** A gap in the trees. Distant castle windows. The forest vast around a small boy and a centaur.
- **Who:** Firenze, profile, blond hair, bow, looking toward Hogwarts. Harry beside the palomino shoulder, looking the same way, suddenly seeing the castle as a vault.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the castle, troubled for the world, not only the boy. Harry looks at the lights, the dog, the package, the book, all connecting. His face is pale and certain.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "He walks these trees because of the Philosopher's Stone. It is hidden in your school."
#### C15-P4-P5

- **Beat:** The Stone would give Voldemort a body without the curse.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands open and close. He understands the theft. Not gold. A body. A return. The hood would not need unicorn blood if it had the Stone. He looks at his own hands, the boy who already survived that return once.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon. Close on him, Firenze a tall presence at the edge.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, cloak, empty hands, the understanding. Firenze watching him, not interrupting.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, then at the scar, fear and a fierce no. Firenze looks at him, respect, a child who understood quickly. Sadness, because understanding is not safety.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "The Stone would give him a body. He would not need the curse of the blood." **Firenze:** "You understand quickly. I am sorry that you do."
#### C15-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Castle lights ahead. He looks at his friends, sure now that Snape serves the hood.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They have reached the forest edge. Hermione and Neville and Hagrid run up, lanterns bouncing. Draco is already there, pale, not meeting eyes. Harry looks at his friends and does not say the centaur’s whole speech. His face says he has a villain. He is wrong about the face, and he is sure.
- **Setting:** The forest edge, the castle blazing with ordinary windows, safety looking close and false. Night. Mist.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak torn, glasses, scar, Firenze already fading back into the trees, a nod goodbye. Hermione reaching him, hair, lantern, fear for him. Neville behind her, crying with relief. Hagrid, huge, crossbow, devastated that Harry was alone. Draco apart, cloak muddy, looking at the ground. Fang licking Harry’s hand.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hermione, alive, and past her toward the castle, the theory locked: Snape. Hermione looks at Harry, scanning him for wounds. Hagrid looks at the trees, guilty. Draco looks at no one, shame. Firenze looks back once, unreadable, and the dark takes him.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Harry. Are you hurt?" **Harry:** "I'm all right. It was Voldemort. And Snape is helping him. I know it."

## Chapter 17 — They decide to protect the Stone

Exams, Hagrid’s slip, a warning refused, and a flute in a dark corridor.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 5 — The stranger

#### C16-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A man in a pub kept buying the drinks and kept Hagrid talking.
- **Image:** A memory, softer edges. The Hog’s Head or the Leaky Cauldron, a dark corner, a hooded stranger pushing a mug across a wet table. Hagrid, flushed, talking with his hands. Cards or a game nearby. The stranger’s face is in shadow on purpose.

- **Who:** Memory-Hagrid, coat, beard, too many empty mugs, earnest. The stranger, hood up, hands pale, one hand pushing a drink, face hidden. Not clearly turban or Snape. Ambiguous, which is honest to Hagrid’s memory.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the stranger, lonely and glad of the company, unsuspecting. The stranger looks down, listening too well. We do not get eyes.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Fluffy's mine. Raised him from a pup. Three heads, and gentle if you know the trick." **Stranger:** "Another firewhisky. Do tell me about the school."
#### C16-P2-P2

- **Setting:** The pub table, close, memory.
- **Who:** The stranger’s hands, hood edge. Hagrid leaning in, fond of his dog.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks proud, a little drunk, about to boast. The hood tilts, hungry for the answer. No smile we can trust.

On the truth side of the panel, unseen by Harry: Quirrell’s purple turban and nervous hands pushing the drink, the hood down a little. A line between them. Harry is looking at the wrong half.
- **Setting:** The hut’s air, a vision panel, summer dust.

- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Snape. It was Snape in that pub. It has to be."

- **Who:** The three, small, Harry’s fingers on the flute, Hermione’s wand light starting, Ron’s shoulders up. The door. Fluffy unseen, announced.

- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "He's awake." **Hermione:** "Then play, Harry. Play now."

## Chapter 18 — Through the Trapdoor

A sleeping dog, a strangling plant, a flock of keys, a chess sacrifice, and a logic problem with only one swallow forward.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 2 — Fluffy and the snare

#### C17-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Harry plays, badly. The heads droop one at a time.
- **Image:** The door is open. Fluffy fills the room again, three heads snarling — then the flute’s thin tune wobbles out and the left head blinks, heavy. The middle head resists. The right head yawns, enormous teeth and a pink tongue. Harry’s fingers stumble and he keeps going.
- **Setting:** The third-floor room, night, moonlight from a high slit, stone, the trapdoor under the paws. The flute is a small sweet sound in a huge smell of dog.
- **Who:** Fluffy, three heads, shaggy, collars, drool, the left eye already shut. Harry in front, robes, glasses, flute at his mouth, cheeks puffed, scar. Hermione and Ron pressed to the door behind him, wands lit, tiny.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the heads, concentrated, not a musician, a boy who will not stop. The middle head looks at him, suspicious, then glassy. Hermione looks at the paws, waiting for the moment. Ron looks at the teeth, pale, staying.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's meant to be a lullaby. Please work." **Hermione:** "One head is down. Don't stop."
#### C17-P1-P2

- **Beat:** They haul a head aside and drop through the trapdoor.
- **Image:** One huge head is a snoring weight they roll by the ear. The trapdoor ring is free. Hermione lifts it. A dark square. They look at each other and drop, Harry last, the flute tucked in his belt. A snore follows them down.
- **Setting:** The room above, the square of darkness below. Moonlight on the dog’s closed eyes. No ladder. A fall.
- **Who:** Hermione lifting the ring, robes, hair tied, determined. Ron sitting on the edge, freckles, red hair, swinging his legs in, scared and going. Harry, flute in his belt, glasses, one look back at Fluffy to be sure. The dog, asleep, three tongues out, ridiculous and lethal.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks down, a swallow, then she goes. Ron looks at Harry, a crooked brave grin. Harry looks at the sleeping heads, a flash of thanks to Hagrid’s music, then he jumps. Fluffy does not look at anything.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "No ladder. Brilliant." **Harry:** "Jump."
#### C17-P1-P3

- **Beat:** They land in something soft. It feels lucky.
- **Image:** They hit a mass of pale vines and sink, laughing once with relief. It is soft. It is dark. Harry’s glasses are askew and he is grinning. For one panel the fall was the danger and the landing is kind.
- **Setting:** A pit under the trapdoor. Wand-light. Thick pale plants, leaves like fingers, a dirt smell, no floor visible. The trapdoor a small square of moonlight far above.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione waist-deep in vines, robes rumpled, wands up, the flute still in Harry’s belt. They look like children who got away with something.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, a breathless laugh. Ron looks at the leaves, pleased. Hermione looks at the vines, the laugh dying first, she recognizes them. Her face changes before theirs.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Soft landing. We are the luckiest idiots in Hogwarts."
#### C17-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Vines tighten. Ron is pulled under.
- **Image:** The plants wake. Loops cinch. Ron yelps and is yanked down to his chest, then his shoulders. A vine crosses his mouth. Harry grabs his wrist and is pulled too. The softness was a mouth.
- **Setting:** The pit, wand-light swinging, leaves wrapping, moonlight far above useless.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, eyes huge, one arm up, the rest disappearing into pale green. Harry, both hands on that wrist, glasses, robes caught. Hermione higher on a thicker stem, one arm free, wand in it. Devil’s Snare, many fingers, no face, patient.
- **Faces:** Ron looks up at Harry, panic, he cannot shout. Harry looks at Ron’s disappearing shoulder, fear, pulling. Hermione looks at the plant, thinking hard, afraid and angry at it. The plant has no face. Its grip is the expression.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "It's got me! Harry, it's pulling me under!"
#### C17-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione shouts that Devil’s Snare hates light.
- **Image:** She has it. Her mouth is open on the name. Her wand is already turning. Harry looks at her, lost, still pulling. Ron’s fingers slip in his. The knowledge is a light before the spell is.
- **Setting:** The pit, dark leaves, her face the brightest thing.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair escaping the tie, robes, wand up, the answer in her whole posture. Harry straining. Ron’s hand, freckled, weakening.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the vines, fierce, certain. She looks at the boys, a command in her eyes: do not fight it, I am here. Harry looks at her, trusting. Ron’s eyes look at her, the panic pausing half a second.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Devil's Snare! Devil's Snare hates light and warmth. Stop struggling, Ron!"

### Page 5 — The keys

#### C17-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A room of wings. Hundreds of keys batter the air.
- **Image:** The next chamber is tall and bright with motion. Hundreds of winged keys whirl, gold and silver and brass, a storm with teeth. The door on the far side is locked. A broom leans by the near door, waiting for a Seeker.
- **Setting:** A stone room, high windows or none, torch brackets, the air itself occupied. The locked door, old and heavy, a keyhole.
- **Who:** The keys, small metal bodies, delicate wings, hostile. Harry, Ron, Hermione in the doorway, leaves still in their hair, looking up. The broom, old, school-issued, against the wall.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the broom, recognition, the courtyard and the pitch in his hands already. Ron looks at the flock, ducking a key. Hermione looks at the lock, then at Harry, she knows this one is his. A key looks, somehow, vicious.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Winged keys. The door is locked. One of them fits the keyhole."
#### C17-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Harry takes the broom without being asked.
- **Image:** He is astride before the plan is spoken. Hermione opens her mouth and closes it. Ron hands him nothing; there is nothing to hand. The flock rustles, interested.
- **Setting:** The near wall. The broom. The storm of keys above.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, leaves in his hair, hands right on the broom, the flute no longer the point. Hermione, a half-gesture of be careful. Ron, a thumbs-up he immediately regrets as uncool and leaves up anyway.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the lock, then at the flock, calm the way he is only in the air. Hermione looks at him, trust. Ron looks at him, pride. Harry does not look back for long. The job is up.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "That's a broom. This room was built for a Seeker."
#### C17-P2-P3

- **Beat:** He flies. The keys swirl like a hostile flock.
- **Image:** He kicks off and the room becomes sky. Keys dive at his face. He banks. A silver one clips his ear. The flock turns as a school of fish. He is the only living shape among metal wings.
- **Setting:** The full height of the room. Torches. The locked door small below. Ron and Hermione tiny, shouting clues he half hears.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet-less school robes streaming anyway, glasses, broom, a line of blood starting at his ear or hand. The keys, a whirlwind. His friends, two upturned faces.
- **Faces:** Harry looks through the flock, searching, not swatting, Seeker-eyes. Pain flickers and gets filed. Hermione looks up, tracking a large old key with her finger. Ron looks up, flinching for him.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Left! No, your other left!"
#### C17-P2-P4

- **Beat:** One old silver key, bent wing.
- **Image:** There. Among the bright new keys, one heavy old silver key with a bent wing, favoring one side, circling near the door it belongs to. Hermione’s pointing finger and Harry’s gaze meet on it. It knows it has been seen and darts.
- **Setting:** Mid-air, the flock parting for one second around the old key. Torchlight on silver.
- **Who:** The old key, tarnished, one wing crooked, larger than the others. Harry banking hard toward it. Hermione below, arm straight, she found it. Ron hopping.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the bent wing, focus narrowing to a point. The key, if metal can panic, panics. Hermione looks at Harry, a fierce go. Her face is a teammate’s.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "The old one! Silver, with the bent wing. That's the key!"
#### C17-P2-P5

- **Beat:** It fights and cuts his hand. His fist closes on it.
- **Image:** The catch is not clean. The key rakes his palm, a bright cut, wings beating against his knuckles. He closes his fist anyway. Blood and silver. He pulls up before the wall.
- **Setting:** Close to the far wall, the locked door under him, torches.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, the key crushed carefully inside it, wings still struggling, blood between his fingers. His face, glasses, a grimace that is also a grin. The broom.
- **Faces:** He looks at his fist, pained, certain he has the right one. The key does not stop fighting. He looks like the boy who caught the Remembrall, older by a year of Saturdays.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Got you. Stop cutting me."
#### C17-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The lock turns. They run through before the flock regroups.
- **Image:** He is down, the key in the lock, the door swinging. The flock, cheated, dives as one. Hermione yanks Ron through. Harry kicks the door shut. A dozen keys ping off the wood. Silence, then their breath.
- **Setting:** The doorway between rooms. Behind, the angry metallic storm. Ahead, a cold quiet and a chessboard glimpsed.
- **Who:** Harry, bleeding hand, broom left behind, glasses, shoulder to the shut door. Hermione pulling Ron. Ron, red hair, a key-cut on his cheek, laughing once in shock. The old key, spent, in the lock.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hand, then at them, a shaky smile. Hermione looks at the cut, already thinking of the next room, a thank-you in the look. Ron looks at the door, giddy to be unpecked. They run forward.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Through, before the flock comes back."

### Page 7 — The chessboard

#### C17-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Giant chess. The pieces are stone and armed.
- **Image:** A hall-sized board. The squares are the floor. The pieces are taller than the children, carved stone, swords and halberds real enough. They turn their heads as the three enter. A king. A queen with a blank, terrible face. The far door is beyond the last rank.
- **Setting:** A cold chamber, black and white squares, torchlight, no windows. Echo. The pieces’ shadows are longer than they are.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, small at the edge, leaves and cuts and wands. The white pieces nearest, a pawn’s stone eyes rolling down. The black pieces waiting on the far side, hostile.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the board, and for the first time tonight he looks at home. The fear is still there. The understanding is bigger. Harry looks at Ron, seeing it. Hermione looks at the queen, wary. A pawn looks at them, impassive, waiting to be commanded or to crush them.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Wizard's chess. Life size. Don't step on a square until I say so."
#### C17-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Ron walks the board the way other people read. He places his friends.
- **Image:** Ron steps onto the squares without flinching. He points Harry to a bishop’s empty place, Hermione to a rook’s. He talks with his hands, the fever and the jokes gone, a commander. The pieces allow it. One knight steps aside for him.
- **Setting:** The board, mid-field, torches, the huge pieces making aisles.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater under robes, freckles, red hair, bandaged no longer, the bitten hand gesturing, sure. Harry moving to a bishop square, trusting. Hermione to a castle square, a book-person suddenly a piece, accepting it. A stone knight, horse-head, stepping back.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the whole board, not at his fear. Calm, bright, the best of him. Harry looks at Ron, respect, he will go where he is told. Hermione looks at Ron, a new understanding, she is not the clever one in this room. The knight looks at Ron, as if recognizing a player.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Harry, you're a bishop. Diagonals only. Hermione, you're the castle, the rook, straight lines. I'll take the knight."
#### C17-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry a bishop. Hermione a castle. Ron takes a knight.
- **Image:** They are in place. Harry stands on a bishop’s square, diagonal world. Hermione on a rook’s, straight lines. Ron swings onto the stone knight’s back, small on the carved horse. The black pieces stir. The game can start.
- **Setting:** The board from slightly above, so the squares read. Three children among giants.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bleeding hand, on his square, wand in his sleeve. Hermione, hair, robes, serious. Ron astride the knight, red hair a flag, freckles, a nod to begin. Stone pieces, armed.
- **Faces:** Ron looks across at the black king, already ten moves ahead. Harry looks at Ron, waiting for the order. Hermione looks along her line, ready to move when told. She looks like she hates not knowing the plan and trusts it anyway.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "White plays. Do exactly as I call it. These pieces do not take prisoners."
#### C17-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Pieces smash each other apart. Dust and swords.
- **Image:** The game is violent. A black pawn explodes into rubble under a white piece’s sword. Dust rolls. Hermione’s rook charges and she hangs on, hair whipping, a shout of effort. A stone head rolls past Harry’s foot. This is not a classroom board.
- **Setting:** The board in chaos. Torchlight through dust. Cracks in the floor. The far door still shut.
- **Who:** Hermione riding or running her piece, fierce, a scrape on her cheek. Harry moved diagonal, a bishop’s lunge, glasses dusty. Ron on the knight, shouting a move, in his element and terrified. Stone soldiers breaking.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the wreckage, calculating, a wince when a piece dies, he feels it. Hermione looks at the next square, determined, not screaming. Harry looks at Ron for the next order, a soldier. The black queen turns her blank face toward Ron’s knight.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Hermione, two squares straight. Harry, diagonal, now. Don't you flinch."
#### C17-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Ron sees the only win and rides forward to be taken.
- **Image:** Quiet inside the noise. Ron sees it. His face goes still. He looks at Harry, then at the queen, then he smiles a small, crooked, serious smile and puts his heels to the knight. He rides into the square where the queen can take him. He does not look like a boy messing about. He looks like a choice.
- **Setting:** The board, dust settling for one second. The queen’s shadow over his square. The far door close now.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, maroon sleeves, on the knight, small. The black queen, tall, sword, blank face, turning toward him. Harry several squares away, realizing too late. Hermione further, a cry starting.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, the smile, a go-on already in it. Love and terror and pride. Harry looks at him, no, the word not out yet. The queen looks at Ron, nothing human, the sword rising. Hermione looks at Ron, understanding a second after Harry, horror.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "There's only one win. The queen has to take me. Harry, when she does, you move on the king. Checkmate." **Harry:** "Ron, no." **Ron:** "That's an order, bishop."
#### C17-P3-P6

- **Beat:** The queen strikes him down. He has already told them to go on.
- **Image:** The sword comes down. Ron is flung from the knight, a small body on the stone, still. Dust. The king is in check from the move he bought; Harry’s square is the mate, and he will have to take it. Ron’s eyes are closed. His hand is open toward the far door.
- **Setting:** The board. Rubble. Torch. The door beyond the king.
- **Who:** Ron on the stones, red hair fanned, freckles, a bruise already, breathing — he is alive and out. The queen, toppled or spent by the sequence, not the focus. Harry on his bishop square, the winning move under his feet, looking back. Hermione running toward Ron and stopping because the game is not over until they move.
- **Faces:** Ron’s face is slack, a ghost of the smile, unconscious. Harry looks at him, wrecked, then at the door, because Ron already told him. Hermione looks at Ron, tears she blinks away, then at Harry, we have to finish. Grief and obedience.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Go on. Both of you. Checkmate, then the door. I'm all right. Go."

- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair singed at one tip, a scratch, robes, empty hands, the respect of not touching until she knows. Harry a soft presence at the edge, watching her work.

- **Setting:** The table, top-down feeling, seven bottles, two chosen.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hands, ink from another life under the dust, sure. Harry’s hand not touching any bottle. The two chosen vials, one clear and cold-looking, one tiny and bright.

- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I am not the one who should walk through that door. You are. I'll take the way back to Ron." **Harry:** "Hermione." **Hermione:** "Drink. And don't you dare waste a drop."
#### C17-P4-P6

- **Image:** Page-turn. He drinks the swallow. Cold fire. The iron door opens on black. He steps through. It begins to close. Through the narrowing gap, Hermione’s face, one hand raised, not quite a wave, the other bottle clutched. Then the door shuts. He is alone.
- **Setting:** The threshold. Behind her, bottles and torch. Ahead of him, darkness, a hint of a tall frame that might be a mirror. The door, iron, closing.
- **Who:** Harry, alone on the far side, the taste of the potion, glasses, scar, empty tiny bottle dropped. Hermione on the near side, reaching, then gone as the door meets stone.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at him, brave, eyes bright, stay alive. Harry looks at her, the same, a small nod. When the door shuts, his face is alone in the dark, the nod unfinished. The year of three becomes one.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "We will be behind you as soon as Ron can stand." **Harry:** "I know."

## Chapter 19 — The Man with Two Faces

The mirror, the wrong teacher, a lie, a burning touch, and a collapse.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

Voldemort is hidden on the far side of the skull, under the cloth, and is not drawn. Harry, small, dusty, the cut hand, glasses, scar prickling.
- **Faces:** Quirrell’s own face looks at Harry, polite, almost friendly, a cold intelligence. No stutter in the mouth. It is not Voldemort’s face. Harry looks at him, the year’s theory collapsing. Shock. He looks at the turban. Oh.

Harry looks at the floor, ashamed and reeling, the hatred he spent all year mis-aimed. He looks younger. He looks angry at himself, then back at Quirrell’s face, the anger finding the right target.
