- **Who:** Harry’s hand, cut palm, pressing the outside of the pocket. The Stone, a round hardness, not shown naked, felt. Quirrell’s robe at the edge, not touching skin.
- **Faces:** Harry’s face above, a flash of wonder he crushes into stillness. He looks at his own hand, then schools his face. He looks like a terrible liar about to try. The scar hurts. He ignores it.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It came to me because I want to find the Stone, not use it."
#### C18-P2-P6

- **Beat:** He lies, clearly. He only sees himself winning the House Cup and shaking Dumbledore’s hand.
- **Image:** Harry turns from the glass, a bright, bad, child’s lie on his face. His hand stays over the pocket. Side view of one head: Quirrell’s face leans toward Harry, eager. On the opposite side of the same skull, Voldemort’s face looks the other way and still seems to hear the lie. Not a face around the cheek. Not two men.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Mirror behind Harry. Torches. The glass is not showing the parents.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, glasses, the lie in his posture, pocket guarded. Quirrell bent down, purple robes, turban off, his own pale face toward Harry. Voldemort’s face on the bald back of the head: flat white-gray, slit nostrils, red eyes, lipless mouth, no hair, no body. The red Stone hidden.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Quirrell’s face, earnest, lying. Quirrell’s face looks at him, wanting to believe, frustrated. Voldemort’s face, on the far side of the skull, is not a second listener standing beside them. It does not believe. Harry is a bad liar and the picture makes that clear.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I see myself winning the House Cup. I shake Professor Dumbledore's hand. That's the whole wish. There is no Stone."

### Page 5 — The face

#### C18-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Voldemort says the boy lies.
- **Image:** Side view of the one head. Voldemort’s face, on the back of the bald scalp, snarls. Quirrell’s own face is the other side of that skull, twisted, obedient, and his hand shoots toward the pocket. Harry twists away. Not two men lunging. The lie is over.
- **Setting:** The mirror foot. Torches flaring. Close, violent, no room. No parents in the glass.
- **Who:** One Quirrell, purple robes, turban off, pale hands. Voldemort only as the face on the scalp: flat white-gray, red eyes, slit nostrils, lipless mouth. Harry turning, pocket side away, wand up, glasses crooked.
- **Faces:** Voldemort’s face looks toward Harry, hatred, certainty. Quirrell’s face, opposite side of the skull, looks at the pocket, desperate. Harry looks at the hand coming, fear and refusal. He will not give it over.
- **Dialogue:** **Voldemort:** "He lies. The Philosopher's Stone is in his pocket."
#### C18-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Quirrell grabs Harry’s arm. The hands smoke.
- **Image:** Skin meets skin. Quirrell’s fingers lock on Harry’s bare wrist where the sleeve rode up. Smoke jumps from the grip. A scream. The Stone is still in the pocket. The touch itself is the weapon, and it is not Harry’s wand.
- **Setting:** Extreme close. Wrist, fingers, smoke, torchlight. The mirror a gold smear behind.
- **Who:** Quirrell’s hand, pale, already blistering, locked on Harry’s wrist. Harry’s arm, thin, a child’s, the scar not here but the mother’s protection in the skin. Smoke, not fire from a wand.
- **Faces:** Not fully in frame. Harry’s mouth at the edge, shocked by the scream that is not his. The face we see on the head, if it is in frame, is Quirrell’s own face, agony, looking at his smoking hand. Voldemort stays on the far side of the skull and is not drawn on this wrist. No second man.
- **Dialogue:** **Quirrell:** "Give it to me." **Harry:** "Let go. Your hand is smoking."
#### C18-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Quirrell screams and lets go. The skin is blistered.
- **Image:** Side view of one head. He reels back, holding his burned hand with the other. Blisters. Smoke. Harry staggers free, wrist red but whole. Quirrell’s own face stares at the burned palm. On the opposite side of the same skull, Voldemort’s face is a thinner scream. Not a second man.
- **Setting:** The chamber, a step of space between them, torches, the mirror.
- **Who:** Side view of one head. Quirrell, purple robes, burned hand outstretched, his own pale face looking at the palm. On the opposite side of the same skull, Voldemort’s face, flat white-gray, red eyes, slit nostrils, lipless, twisted in rage. No second body. Harry, wrist clutched, pocket still heavy, glasses, dusty, wand unused.
- **Faces:** Quirrell’s face looks at his palm, agony. Voldemort’s face, on the back of the head, looks the other way, hate. Harry looks at his own wrist, stunned, then the stun becomes a choice. He is not looking at two men.
- **Dialogue:** **Quirrell:** "What is this magic? What have you done to my skin?"
#### C18-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort shrieks at him to hold on.
- **Image:** Side view. Voldemort’s face, on the back of the bald head, is all mouth and command. Quirrell’s own face is the other side of that skull. He reaches again because he is more afraid of the face on his own scalp than of the burn. Harry sets his feet. He has decided to be touched. Not two men.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Harsh light. The mirror witnessing, and it does not show Lily and James.
- **Who:** One Quirrell, purple robes, turban off, both hands reaching, one already burned. Voldemort only as the face on the scalp, same white-gray face, red eyes, slit nostrils, lipless mouth. Harry, small, sleeves pushed up on purpose, glasses, scar white, pocket guarded by his body.
- **Faces:** Voldemort’s face looks out from the back of the head, contempt for the pain. Quirrell’s face, opposite side, looks toward Harry, terror and obedience. Harry looks at the reaching hands, afraid and resolved. His chin is down. He will grab the face on the scalp.
- **Dialogue:** **Voldemort:** "Seize him. Hold the boy. I do not care if it burns you."
#### C18-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Harry grabs the face and the arm on purpose. Both of them burn.
- **Image:** Camera on the back of the bald head. Harry lunges. One hand on Quirrell’s wrist. The other hand is on Voldemort’s face, the face embedded in the scalp. Smoke erupts from both contacts. Quirrell’s own face is on the far side of the skull and is not in frame. Harry does not let go.
- **Setting:** The center of the chamber. Smoke. Torches. The mirror flaring white for a second. No grace, no duel poses. One body.
- **Who:** Harry, small, both hands locked on, glasses askew, scar blazing. Quirrell, buckling, purple robes, bald scalp. Under Harry’s palm: Voldemort’s face, flat white-gray, slit nostrils, red eyes, lipless mouth, no hair, no body, smoking. Not a mask and not a second man.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the face on the scalp, teeth set. That face looks back from under the hand, rage and, for the first time, fear. Quirrell’s face is not visible. The scream is his, off-camera.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "You will not have it. You will not have the Stone."
#### C18-P3-P6

- **Beat:** The Stone is a hard point in the pocket. Harry does not let go.
- **Image:** Amid the smoke, a small still detail: the pocket, the round hardness of the Stone against Harry’s hip, safe. His hands are locked above. He is failing and winning in the same picture. His knees buckle and he hangs on by the grip itself.
- **Setting:** Smoke, firelight, the chamber floor coming closer as they sag.
- **Who:** Harry’s torso, the pocket, his hands on Quirrell. One hand is still on the face on the back of the bald head. Quirrell folding. One body.
- **Faces:** Harry’s face, close, eyes shutting, opening, shutting. Pain. Stubbornness. The eye still visible under his hand is Voldemort’s, red, on the scalp, afraid. Quirrell’s own face is not in this crop.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 6 — Collapse

#### C18-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Quirrell comes apart under Harry’s hands.
- **Image:** Where Harry holds, Quirrell’s form fails, not gore for its own sake: ash, smoke, a man losing the shape of his arms. The purple robe empties at the edges. Harry is still attached, horrified, unable to release because letting go might let the face go free.
- **Setting:** The chamber, smoke thick, the mirror a pale rectangle, torches guttering.
- **Who:** Harry, small, coughing, hands locked, glasses dirty, scar a white line. Side view of one failing head: Quirrell’s own eye on one side, wide; on the opposite side of the same skull, Voldemort’s face peeling toward smoke. Same white-gray face, red eyes, no body of his own. Purple robe emptying at the edges.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, horror. Quirrell’s face, one side of the skull, is agony. Voldemort’s face, the other side, looks toward escape. Not two dying men.
- **Dialogue:** **Quirrell:** "Master, I cannot hold him!"
#### C18-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The face on the back of the head is furious and afraid.
- **Image:** Close on the back of the bald head as it fails. The face in frame is Voldemort’s, embedded in the scalp: flat white-gray, slit nostrils, red eyes, lipless mouth, no hair. Fury, and under it fear. Quirrell’s face is not in this close-up. Harry’s palm does not let go.
- **Setting:** Smoke. No room. Just that face on the scalp and Harry’s palm. No second body.
- **Who:** Voldemort’s face on the scalp, and Harry’s hand, small, burning. Not a separate standing Voldemort.
- **Faces:** That face looks at Harry, hate and fear in equal measure. Harry’s eyes, at the edge, look back, crying, refusing. Quirrell’s face stays out of frame.
- **Dialogue:** **Voldemort:** "Do not fail me, Quirrell. I will not end in this room."
#### C18-P4-P3

- **Beat:** The scar is white-hot. Harry’s vision tunnels.
- **Image:** From inside Harry’s eyes. The room narrows to a circle. Smoke at the edges. The face in the center, then blurring. The scar’s pain is a white band across the top of the panel. His hands are still in the circle. Just.
- **Setting:** Subjective. The chamber reduced to a porthole of torch and smoke.
- **Who:** Harry’s view of the back of Quirrell’s bald head. The face in the center is Voldemort’s, the same flat white-gray face, red eyes, slit nostrils, lipless mouth, on the scalp. Not Quirrell’s face. His own hands, failing. No friends. No Dumbledore yet.
- **Faces:** We see Voldemort’s face raging on the back of the head. We feel Harry’s face from the way the world shakes. He is going under. The Stone is still a hard truth in the blur.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C18-P4-P4

- **Beat:** He hears his own name in Dumbledore’s voice, far off.
- **Image:** The circle of vision is almost black. A voice, not a body: his name, calm, the headmaster’s. A hint of purple stars and silver beard at the edge of the dark, maybe real, maybe the last thing he wants. His fingers loosen.
- **Setting:** Near-black. A rim of torch. A suggestion of a tall figure arriving through smoke, not fully drawn, so we stay with Harry’s fading.
- **Who:** Harry’s hands falling. The voice. Perhaps Dumbledore’s hand entering the dark, long fingers, a ring, not clearly seen. Quirrell a heap of smoke and robe.
- **Faces:** No clear face. The feeling on Harry is relief so strong it looks like surrender. If Dumbledore’s eye is there, it looks at the boy, urgent, gentle, too late to stop the fainting and in time for the rest.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Harry. Harry, hold on."
#### C18-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Quirrell falls. A dark smoke tears loose and flees through Harry.
- **Image:** The body collapses, a ruined robe. A stream of black smoke rips free, passes through Harry’s chest like a cold wind, and shoots toward the ceiling and away. It has no face now. It is escape. Harry’s hair lifts. His pocket stays heavy. The smoke does not take the Stone.
- **Setting:** The chamber, clearing slightly, the mirror, the fallen robe, a crack of air.
- **Who:** Quirrell’s empty robes, still. The smoke, a fleeing streak. Harry, upright for one last second, arms out, glasses, scar, the Stone in the pocket, a hole of cold where the smoke passed.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the smoke, fading, a question he cannot hold. The smoke has no face. It looks like flight. His own face is white, the stubbornness used up.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C18-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Harry hits the floor. The mirror shows an empty room.
- **Image:** Page-turn. He falls. The Stone is a small lump under him, safe against the stone floor and his robe. Dust. His glasses skid. In the mirror, for one cruel or kind second, the room is empty: no parents, no Cup, no thief. Just a fallen boy, and then the mirror goes ordinary. Silence.
- **Setting:** The chamber floor. Torches steadying. The mirror. Smoke thinning. No Quirrell standing. No friends yet. Dumbledore’s hem perhaps at the edge, a promise, not a rescue pose.
- **Who:** Harry, face-down or on his side, glasses off, scar quiet, dusty, burned-but-whole hands, the pocket between him and the floor. The mirror. The empty robes.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are closed. Peace, finally, not death if we can help the next chapter, but the panel does not wink. He looks young. The mirror shows no desire. It shows a boy who did the thing and stopped. The room looks at him. No one in the glass waves.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

## Chapter 20 — The hospital wing and the explanation

Bandages, a destroyed Stone, a mother’s protection, an unanswered question, and chocolate.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

### Page 1 — The white corridor

#### W20-01-P1

- **Beat:** Summer outside a tall window.
- **Image:** The castle in summer daylight, a high hospital window, white curtain moving. The grounds are green. The crisis is over and the boy is not awake.
- **Setting:** Outside the hospital wing. Summer.
- **Who:** The window. No face.
- **Faces:** The day looks kind. The window looks quiet.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
#### W20-01-P2

- **Beat:** The corridor of the wing.
- **Image:** A clean corridor, Madam Pomfrey far down it with a trolley, not at the bed. A door to the ward. Dumbledore's hat is a starry point already inside, seen through the crack. He is waiting. He has not greeted anyone.
- **Setting:** The hospital corridor. Day.
- **Who:** Madam Pomfrey in the distance. Dumbledore's hat through the door.
- **Faces:** Pomfrey looks busy. The hat looks patient.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
#### W20-01-P3

- **Beat:** The ward, whole, and one boy asleep.
- **Image:** White curtains, empty wooden beds, flowers, a jug of water, summer light. Harry in a pale shirt, both hands bandaged, hair flat on one side, asleep. Dumbledore sits in the chair, very tall, purple and stars, half-moon spectacles, not speaking.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, summer day.
- **Who:** Dumbledore and sleeping Harry. No Ron. No Hermione. They are not in the room yet.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks fond and tired. Harry looks young and gone.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
#### W20-01-P4

- **Beat:** He was already waiting.
- **Image:** Dumbledore's hands folded on the cane or the knee, beard, the chair too small. A box of sweets is not out yet. The earwax is later. Here he only waits.
- **Setting:** The bedside.
- **Who:** Dumbledore beside the bed.
- **Faces:** He looks as if he has been there a long while and does not mind.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.
#### W20-01-P5

- **Beat:** Pomfrey looks in and leaves them.
- **Image:** She glances, approves the quiet, and goes. The curtain falls. Dumbledore does not turn the visit into a staff meeting.
- **Setting:** The wing.
- **Who:** Madam Pomfrey in the door. Dumbledore. Harry asleep.
- **Faces:** She looks professional and fond. He looks grateful and still.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

- **Beat:** Harry's eyelids move.
- **Image:** The bandaged hands on the sheet. His eyes have not opened. The next old panel is the waking, white curtains, sun. Here, the moment before.
- **Setting:** The bed, the second before he wakes.
- **Who:** Harry's face, scar, bandages. Dumbledore.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore leans a fraction. Harry is on the edge of the room.
- **Dialogue:** None.
- **Art:** Not drawn yet. No picture file. Do not reuse another panel's jpg.

### Page 2 — Waking

#### C19-P1-P1

- **Beat:** White curtains. Sun. Bandaged hands.
- **Image:** He wakes to white. Sun through a high window. Both hands are bandaged, resting on a clean blanket. The underground is gone. A jug of water. The quiet of a school hospital, not a chamber.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, daytime, summer, white curtains moving a little, other beds empty, flowers somewhere. Peace so bright it is suspicious.
- **Who:** Harry, in a hospital shirt, hair flat on one side, glasses set neatly on the side table by someone else, scar pale, bandaged hands. No one else in frame yet. Hedwig is not here. The Stone is not in sight.
- **Faces:** He looks at his hands, groggy, trying to remember why they hurt. Confused. Alive. His eyes move toward the glasses, wanting them, not reaching yet.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C19-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore sits beside the bed as if he has been there a while.
- **Image:** With the glasses on — he has fumbled them up — Harry finds Dumbledore in the chair, long beard, half-moon spectacles, a pile of sweets he is not eating, a book closed on his knee. He looks like he has been guarding the sleep. Sun in his silver hair.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Curtains. A quiet wing. Summer afternoon.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple robes simpler today, or the starry ones, hat off and on his knee under the book, spectacles, beard, hands folded. Harry, bandaged, glasses, hospital shirt, small in the bed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, relief deep under the calm, a smile that is not a joke. Harry looks at him, the voice in the dark connecting to the man. Trust. A boy checking the world is still there. Dumbledore looks a little tired. He has been there a while.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Good afternoon, Harry. I have been waiting for you to come back to us."
#### C19-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Harry’s first reach is for the pocket. It is empty.
- **Image:** His bandaged hand paws the hospital shirt where a pocket is not. Panic. The Stone. He tries to sit. Dumbledore’s hand, gentle, rests on the blanket, not pinning him, steadying. The pocket is empty because there is no pocket. The panic is the first real expression.
- **Setting:** The bed. Sun. The chair.
- **Who:** Harry, struggling up, glasses, bandages, alarm. Dumbledore, one hand on the blanket’s edge, calm.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his own side, frightened, did he fail after all. Dumbledore looks at him, understanding at once, kind. He looks at the empty hand. He is about to give the answer. Harry looks at him, begging for it.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "The Stone. It was in my pocket. Where is it?"
#### C19-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says the Stone is safe, and that it will be destroyed.
- **Image:** He says it simply. Harry sinks back. Safe. Then the second clause. Destroyed. Harry’s face does both: relief, then a question. Dumbledore’s hands open, no Stone in them, nothing to show, the news is the whole picture.
- **Setting:** The quiet wing. A curtain breathes.
- **Who:** The two of them. Harry, bandaged, small. Dumbledore, tall even seated, beard, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at him, relief first, the forest-hood losing. Then confusion about destroyed. Dumbledore looks at Harry, gentle, sure, a little sad for the object and not for the choice. He looks at Harry’s hands, grateful they are the cost and not a grave.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "The Philosopher's Stone is safe. And it is going to be destroyed."
#### C19-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Harry asks why. He is thinking of endless life.
- **Image:** Harry’s question is small. In his eyes, a flicker of the book’s engraving, the red stone, Ron’s open hand, his parents’ faces in a mirror. Endless life. Why break that. He does not say the parents out loud. Dumbledore hears it anyway.
- **Setting:** The bed. Sun. A bowl of grapes or chocolate frogs waiting, ordinary, against the size of the question.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bandaged hands on the blanket, the question. Dumbledore listening, beard, a long patience.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, earnest, a child who has seen what the Stone costs and still asks. Dumbledore looks at him, tender, the mirror-night remembered. He is not angry at the question.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Destroyed? It makes the Elixir of Life. Nicolas Flamel has lived on it for centuries."
#### C19-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Endless life and endless gold are not gifts the world should have.
- **Image:** Dumbledore looks toward the window, the summer grounds, children — tiny, far — dragging trunks, ordinary lives. His answer is quiet. Harry looks at those tiny students. The Stone would have bent all of them. The panel is a window and two faces, not a lecture.
- **Setting:** The hospital window. Lawns. End of term. Sun. Distant laughter, harmless.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, profile, silver, spectacles, sad wisdom. Harry, three-quarter, glasses, following his gaze. Tiny students outside, no faces needed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the children outside, protective of a whole world, not only Harry. Harry looks out, then at Dumbledore, accepting it before he fully understands. A nod, small. His face is tired and older than September.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Endless gold and endless life are not gifts this world ought to keep on a shelf. Nicolas agrees with me. He has had time enough."

### Page 3 — Why Quirrell burned

#### C19-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Harry asks why his touch burned Quirrell.
- **Image:** He holds up the bandaged hands, the evidence. The question is practical and enormous. Dumbledore looks at the bandages as if they are a relic. The wing is very quiet.
- **Setting:** The bedside. White linen. Sun.
- **Who:** Harry, both hands raised a little, bandages, glasses, hospital shirt. Dumbledore, leaning in, spectacles low on his nose.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, uneasy with them, then at Dumbledore. He is afraid the answer will be that he is dangerous. Dumbledore looks at the hands, moved, a shine in the eye he does not hide. He looks at Harry’s face, ready to give him the better truth.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Why did my hands burn Professor Quirrell? I never cast a spell."
#### C19-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore begins to talk about Harry’s mother.
- **Image:** He says her name with care. Harry goes still in the way he did before the mirror. A flower in a vase on the bedside table, ordinary, stands in for tenderness. Dumbledore’s voice is the light in the panel.
- **Setting:** The wing. The flower. Summer.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, hands folded, speaking. Harry, still, glasses, scar, listening with his whole body.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, gentle, choosing words for a child. Harry looks at him, hungry, the mirror-mother already rising. Hope and fear of hearing it wrong. His mouth is closed. He is listening harder than he has listened all year.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Because of your mother, Harry. Lily Potter."
#### C19-P2-P3

- **Beat:** A quiet panel. Lily as light and a shape between a crib and a green curse.
- **Image:** Not a spectacle. A dim nursery. A crib. A young woman with long dark-red hair stands between the crib and a spear of green light. She is seen mostly as a silhouette and a fall of hair, one hand raised, not a portrait to be copied, a feeling: someone stepping into the way. The green stops at her. The baby is only a small dark-haired shape.
- **Setting:** A cottage nursery at night, simplified, memory-soft, not the full ruin. A mobile. A blanket. The green light from a doorway.
- **Who:** Lily, hair, a simple dress or nightclothes, her face turned away or lost in light, one arm out. Baby Harry in the crib, a tuft of black hair. The attacker is not shown. Only the green.
- **Faces:** Her face is not a clear portrait. The love is in the stance, the way she will not step aside. The baby’s face is calm, asleep or just waking. No sneer, no duel. A person choosing.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C19-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Her love left a protection in Harry’s skin.
- **Image:** Back in the hospital. Dumbledore’s finger, very light, indicates Harry’s bandaged hand, not the scar. The protection is in the skin, not the lightning. Harry looks at his hand as if it belongs to her too.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Sun. The flower.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, spectacles, beard, a careful finger. Harry, the bandaged hand between them, glasses, hospital shirt.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the hand, reverent. Harry looks at it, the horror of Quirrell’s burning rewritten. Eyes wet. He looks at Dumbledore, a question and a thank-you mixed, though the thank-you is for her.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "When she died to keep you alive, her love left a protection in your very skin. Old magic. Not a charm I could set down in a lesson."
#### C19-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Quirrell, full of hate and of Voldemort, could not bear that touch.
- **Image:** Dumbledore says it plainly. Harry sees, small and far, the smoke of that chamber, now explained. He pulls the blanket a little higher, not from fear, from the size of it. A child held a monster off with his mother’s love and it looked like burning.
- **Setting:** The wing. Quiet. A curtain.
- **Who:** The two of them. No Quirrell. The explanation is the picture, their faces, his covered hands.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the blanket, overwhelmed, grieving and proud in a private way. Dumbledore looks at him, making sure he hears love and not a weapon. Kind, serious. Harry looks up, and the shame of the burning eases.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Quirrell was full of hatred, and full of Voldemort. He could not bear to touch that protection. So he burned."
#### C19-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry looks at his bandaged hands as if they belong to someone else.
- **Image:** A quiet close-up. The hands on the white blanket. Bandages. A bit of soot still at the wrist the nurse missed. He turns them. They are his. They are also hers. He does not flex them into fists. He rests them.
- **Setting:** Linen. Sun. No one else in the crop.
- **Who:** Harry’s hands. The edge of his chin, glasses, a tear he has not wiped because his hands feel too important to use for that.
- **Faces:** His mouth, visible, is soft. Wonder. Sorrow. He looks at his hands the way he looked at the cloak. Inheritance.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 4 — The question he is not ready for

#### C19-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Harry touches the scar and says it hurts when Voldemort is close.
- **Image:** He forgets the bandages and almost touches the scar with a wrapped finger. He says it as a fact he has been carrying since the forest, the match, the classroom. Dumbledore does not wave it away.
- **Setting:** The bed. Afternoon softening. The wing.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, a bandaged finger near it. Dumbledore, attentive, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, checking if he is allowed to say the name here. He is. His face is serious. Dumbledore looks at the scar, not hungry like Ollivander, not hating like Snape. Studying, gentle. He nods.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "My scar hurts whenever Voldemort is close. It has, all year."
#### C19-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says that is so, and that it is not a small detail.
- **Image:** He nods, once, the confirmation. Then he looks at Harry a long moment, the not-small part in his eyes. Harry straightens, the way he did when he was made Seeker, a burden recognized.
- **Setting:** The chair and the bed. Quiet.
- **Who:** The two. A plate of chocolate frogs now visible, waiting, a child-thing beside a large truth.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, honest, he will not pretend the scar is only a mark. Harry looks back, a little afraid, steadied by being believed. No one smiles. Respect.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Yes. That is so. And it is not a small detail. We shall not pretend that it is."
#### C19-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry asks why Voldemort wanted to kill a baby.
- **Image:** The question is the smallest voice in the chapter and the largest. Harry’s hands are fists in the bandages now. He looks very eleven. Dumbledore’s face goes still. The summer window is too bright.
- **Setting:** The wing. The window. The bed.
- **Who:** Harry, hospital shirt, glasses, the question. Dumbledore, beard, spectacles, a man with an answer he will not spend today.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at him, pleading, plain. He wants the reason the way he wanted the letter. Dumbledore looks at Harry, love and a gate. He looks older. He looks sorry.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Why did he want to kill a baby? Why me, and not some other child?"
#### C19-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Silence, held for a whole panel.
- **Image:** No speech. The curtain moves. A bird crosses the window. Dumbledore’s hands stay folded. Harry waits. The silence is not empty. It is a decision.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, summer, a clock’s tick you could hear if this were sound.
- **Who:** Both of them, unmoving. The chocolate frogs. The flower.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, hope thinning into patience. Dumbledore looks at his own hands, then at the boy, choosing. Sad. Certain. Kind.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
#### C19-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says Harry will know when he is older. Not today.
- **Image:** He leans in and says it, a promise and a refusal. His hand covers Harry’s bandaged fist, brief. Not today. Harry’s face falls and then accepts, because this man has not lied to him about the mirror or the Stone.
- **Setting:** The bedside, close.
- **Who:** Dumbledore’s hand over the bandages. Harry’s face, glasses, scar. The beard. The spectacles.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, loving, immovable. Harry looks at him, disappointed, trusting anyway. A small nod. Childhood extended one more year by an adult who can still do that. His eyes are wet and he is not arguing.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "You will know, Harry, when you are older. Not today. I will not lay that answer on a hospital pillow."
#### W20-18-P1

- **Beat:** A small box, after the hard answers.
- **Image:** The same bed, summer light, bandaged hands. The Stone talk and the mother talk are done or pausing. Dumbledore produces a Bertie Bott's box, not a Chocolate Frog. The frogs can stay later, with the friends.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing. Afternoon. No friends yet.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, half-moon spectacles, purple robes. Harry in the pale shirt, bandages.
- **Faces:** He looks like a man changing the subject with a sweet. Harry looks tired and curious.
- **Dialogue:** None.

- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks mischievous and kind. Harry looks wary, because Ron told him about a train.

### Page 6 — The friends

#### C19-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Ron and Hermione burst in, remember to be quiet, and burst anyway.
- **Image:** The door bangs. Pomfrey hisses. They tiptoe at a run, which is useless. Ron’s head is bandaged. Hermione has a stack of cards and a cake that is leaning. Harry’s face opens all the way.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing door. Sun. A scolding healer. White curtains flying.
- **Who:** Ron, freckles, red hair sticking up around a white bandage, maroon sweater, a grin. Hermione, summer dress or robes, hair, cards, the cake. Madam Pomfrey, apron, finger up, defeated by them. Harry sitting up, glasses, bandaged hands.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, joy, a shout he turns into a whisper and then abandons. Hermione looks at Harry, scanning for damage, then smiling so hard. Harry looks at them, the alone-in-the-chamber feeling breaking. Pomfrey looks at the ceiling, fond, exasperated.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Harry!" **Hermione:** "You're awake. Madam Pomfrey said we were to whisper." **Ron:** "That was a whisper."
#### C19-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Ron’s head is bandaged from the chess piece. He is proud of it.
- **Image:** Ron taps his bandage like a medal. He retells the queen in a small smash of hands, then winces, then grins. Harry laughs, it hurts his ribs, he does not care. Hermione rolls her eyes and lets him have it.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Cards. Cake. Sun.
- **Who:** Ron, the bandage, freckles, proud. Harry, laughing, glasses, hospital shirt. Hermione, arms folded, a smile she is not hiding well.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, proud, needing him to have seen the move even though he was busy almost dying. Harry looks at the bandage, gratitude that hurts, the smile watery. Hermione looks at Ron, fond, she will add the true details later. Friendship, loud.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Chess piece. Right in the head. Worth it. I was brilliant, in case the queen knocked the memory out of you."
#### C19-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says she solved the riddle fast and then could not go with him.
- **Image:** Her voice drops. She looks at her hands, the ones that held the back bottle. She hates that the logic ended in sending him alone. The cake leans. Ron goes quieter.
- **Setting:** The bed. A quieter panel among loud ones.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair, the confession. Harry, bandaged. Ron, bandage, listening.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her hands, guilty, precise about her own limits. Harry looks at her, shaking his head, she is wrong to hate it. Ron looks at her, the chess still in his body, he knows what it is to be the one who stays behind. Kindness, all three.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I solved Snape's riddle quickly enough. Then I had to send you through the door alone. I hated that part."
#### C19-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Harry says she is why he got there. Ron says he is why too. They argue, happy.
- **Image:** Harry points at Hermione with a bandaged hand, then Ron points at himself and nearly pokes his bandage, and they are all talking at once, a happy pile of credit. Cake is threatened. It is the opposite of the chamber.
- **Setting:** The wing. Sun. Pomfrey listening from her office, pretending not to smile.
- **Who:** The three, overlapping, hands, bandages, hair red and brown and black. The cake. The frogs.
- **Faces:** Harry looks from one to the other, insisting, bright. Hermione looks at him, the guilt loosening, a laugh. Ron looks at both, fake-offended, delighted. They look like the trio the door tried to split.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "You are why I reached that room. The logic was yours." **Ron:** "And the chess was mine. We're both why. We can argue it over cake."
#### C19-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Harry tells them it was Quirrell. Both go still, rewriting the year.
- **Image:** He says the name. The happy noise stops. Ron’s hand stays in the air. Hermione’s mind visibly runs back through a turban, a flinch in a corridor, a Halloween faint, a forest hood. The rewrite is in their eyes. Snape, somewhere in the castle, is suddenly a different shape in their story.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Sun still, the mood changed.
- **Who:** Harry, quiet now, glasses, scar, the truth. Ron and Hermione, stilled.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, tired, sure. Hermione looks at the middle distance, rearranging the year, a shiver. Ron looks at Harry, the Snape-theory falling off his face, a silent wow. They look at Harry’s hands. Understanding. No joke.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It was never Snape. It was Professor Quirrell. Voldemort was living on the back of his head, under the turban." **Hermione:** "All year. The flinch in the corridor. The troll. The broom."
#### C19-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The three of them on the bed with chocolate. The lake outside is bright.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They sit on the bed, allowed, a blanket ruined with cake crumbs and chocolate frog cards. Harry in the middle, bandaged hands held out so Ron can unwrap a frog for him. Hermione has her shoes off. Through the window the lake is bright, the castle in summer, a boat memory far and small. They are safe this hour.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, late afternoon, gold, the window, the lake, green grounds. End of the danger, not of the story.
- **Who:** Harry, center, glasses, scar, hospital shirt, a chocolate frog being put in his bandaged hands. Ron, bandage, freckles, maroon sweater, doing the unwrapping, gentle. Hermione, tucked on the other side, a card in her fingers, hair, a smudge of chocolate. The lake outside.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the frog, then at them, content, the unfinished question set down for now. Ron looks at Harry’s hands, careful, joking softly. Hermione looks at the card, then at the lake, a small peaceful smile. They look like children again. The window looks like summer.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Chocolate Frog card. Dumbledore again. The man turns up in pockets." **Hermione:** "Eat. The lake looks like summer. We have earned a quiet hour."

## Chapter 21 — The House Cup

Green and silver, a count, Neville, and the train.

Opening pages walk in before the first line of a new place: outside, then the room, then what people were already doing. Panels coded W are not drawn yet. Older codes stay on the old pictures.

- **Who:** The three. The castle.
- **Faces:** They look like people who know the score and hate it.
- **Dialogue:** None.

### Page 2 — Slytherin’s night

#### C20-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The Great Hall dressed green and silver. Slytherin thinks the Cup is theirs.
- **Image:** The leaving feast. The hall is a wash of green and silver banners, Slytherin colors, because they are ahead. The other tables look slightly muted. Candles. Trunks packed in the dorms. A cup, large and gleaming, waiting by the staff table as if the night is a formality.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall, evening, end of term. Four tables. The enchanted ceiling a clear summer night. Green and silver everywhere, including a drape behind the Cup.
- **Who:** The school in robes. Slytherin table loud and shiny. Draco near the front of it, platinum hair, a green-trimmed robe. Gryffindor quieter: Harry, Ron with a smaller bandage or a healed scar at the hairline, Hermione, Neville further down, Fred and George subdued. Staff at the high table. Dumbledore. McGonagall. Snape, black, a contained satisfaction. Quirrell’s seat empty.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the Cup, already owning it. Snape looks at his house, a thin smile. Harry looks at his plate, the hospital still in his posture, not sulking, just quiet. Hermione looks at the banners, calculating points in her head, unhappy with the math. Neville looks at his pudding.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Green and silver, Potter. The House Cup is Slytherin's. Do try to clap."
#### C20-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Draco lifts a goblet toward Harry.
- **Image:** Across the hall, Draco raises a goblet in a toast that is a taunt. Crabbe and Goyle grin. A few Slytherins follow the gesture. Harry sees it. He does not lift anything back. Ron starts to stand and Hermione’s hand settles him.
- **Setting:** The two tables, candles, green banners, the distance between houses.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, goblet, elegant sneer, green and silver around him. Harry, glasses, scar, a plain goblet untouched. Ron, ears red, freckles, half-risen. Hermione, a hand on his sleeve, composed and annoyed.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, sweet and poisonous. Harry looks back, tired of him, unshaken. Ron looks at Draco, furious. Hermione looks at Ron, not now. Snape, far, looks at the toast, allowing it.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "To Slytherin. And to first years who go adventuring and still come second."
#### C20-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Dumbledore stands. The room settles.
- **Image:** He rises. The hall’s noise thins without a spell. Even Draco’s goblet lowers a fraction. Dumbledore’s hat is off. His hands rest on the table. He looks like the man from the hospital and the man from the mirror room, about to be the headmaster in public.
- **Setting:** The high table. Candles. The Cup. The ceiling’s stars.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple, silver beard, half-moon spectacles, standing. McGonagall seated, emerald, watching him, she may already know. Snape, the smile pausing. The students, a sea of faces turning.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks over the hall, benign, a secret. McGonagall looks at him, a tiny tightness that might be a smile. Draco looks at him, impatient for the Cup. Harry looks at him, remembering a box of frogs and an unfinished answer. Quiet attention.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "One moment, before the puddings vanish. The House Cup has not yet been given."
#### C20-P1-P4

- **Beat:** He says recent events must be recognized. Snape looks sour.
- **Image:** Dumbledore’s hand opens toward the students, recent events. Snape’s face, beside him, goes sour, the contained smile dying. He looks at his plate. He knows points are coming and not for his house. A twitch of the greasy hair as he turns his head a degree away.
- **Setting:** The high table, close enough to read the staff.
- **Who:** Dumbledore speaking, kind, formal. Snape, black, sallow, the sourness. McGonagall, a gleam. Quirrell’s empty chair, a gap.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the Gryffindor table, warm. Snape looks at the cloth, sour, he will endure it. Harry looks at Snape, a complicated feeling now, not the year’s hatred. Hermione looks at Dumbledore, hope restarting in the math.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Certain recent events were never entered in the books. I intend to repair that." **Snape:** "Headmaster."
#### C20-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Points to Hermione, for cool logic under pressure.
- **Image:** Her name. She blinks. The Gryffindor hourglass — jewels in a great glass at the side of the hall — shifts, red stones pouring. Not enough to win. Enough to change the air. She goes red. Ron bangs the table once, delighted for her.
- **Setting:** The hall. The giant hourglasses, four of them, green still far ahead, red climbing a little. Candles.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, robes, frozen. Harry grinning. Ron, bandage or scar, whooping quietly. Neville clapping. The hourglass, red jewels. Dumbledore, the announcement. Draco, goblet stopped.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Dumbledore, stunned, then at her friends, a smile she tries to make modest. It is not modest. Harry looks at her, proud. Ron looks at her, the riddle and the fire and the plant in his cheer. Draco looks at the hourglass, a frown starting.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "To Miss Hermione Granger, for cool logic under the most unpleasant pressure, fifty points to Gryffindor."
#### C20-P1-P6

- **Beat:** The Gryffindor jewels shift. Slytherin is still ahead.
- **Image:** Wide. The four hourglasses. Green is a mountain. Red has grown and is still short. Yellow and blue are out of it. The hall looks at the gap. Slytherin relaxes again. Gryffindor’s cheer falters into hope that knows the math. Hermione is already re-adding.
- **Setting:** The side of the hall, the hourglasses tall as students, torch and candle light in the jewels.
- **Who:** The glasses themselves, the main characters. Tiny faces: Draco soothed, Harry watching, Hermione calculating, Snape’s sourness easing, Dumbledore not finished, a twinkle.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the green glass, the smirk returning. Hermione looks at the gap, biting her lip. Harry looks at Dumbledore, waiting. Dumbledore looks at the hall, not done. The jewels look like a verdict that is still moving.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Still short of Slytherin." **Hermione:** "I know the sum. Don't cheer yet."

### Page 3 — The count

- **Setting:** Gryffindor table, Neville’s seat, often at the edge, now the center of the panel.
- **Who:** Neville, sandy hair, round face, robes a bit crooked, spoon in midair, Trevor a lump in a pocket. Harry, Ron, Hermione turning toward him. The hourglass beginning to pour.
- **Faces:** Neville looks up, pure bewilderment. A blob of cream on his chin. Harry looks at him, a grin starting, yes. Hermione looks at him, remembering a night he tried to stop them, understanding before Neville does. Ron looks at him, delighted. Neville looks at Dumbledore, is it a mistake.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "To Mr Neville Longbottom. Ten points."
#### C20-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Neville drops his goblet.
- **Image:** The goblet tips, pumpkin juice, a small disaster, very Neville. He does not catch it. His hands are open. His face is still on the headmaster. The juice is funny and nobody is laughing at him. They are cheering toward him.
- **Setting:** The table, spilled juice, candles reflected in it.
- **Who:** Neville, goblet falling, sandy hair, ears going red, robes. Seamus or Dean beside him, laughing kindly, thumping him. Harry leaning in.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at Dumbledore, lost, then at his friends, the start of belief. Embarrassed. Bright. Harry looks at the spill, then at Neville’s face, fond. The table looks at Neville, welcoming him into the noise.
- **Dialogue:** **Neville:** "Me? I didn't do any... oh. The goblet."
#### C20-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Courage to stand up to enemies, and more courage to stand up to friends.
- **Image:** Dumbledore says it, and the panel gives Neville a memory-flash, small: a nightshirt, a portrait hole, Neville in the corridor trying to stop Harry and Ron and Hermione from leaving, scared, doing it anyway. Then back to the feast, the words hanging over him like a banner that fits.
- **Setting:** The hall, and a small soft-edged memory of the Gryffindor corridor at night.
- **Who:** Dumbledore at the high table, speaking. Neville in the present, juice on the table, eyes shining. Memory-Neville, nightshirt, arms spread in a doorway, terrified and brave. Memory-Harry pausing.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Neville, respect, no joke in the sentence. Neville looks at him, the memory hurting in a good way, eyes wet. He looks at his friends. Harry looks at Neville, a nod, you were right to try. Hermione looks at Neville, sorry and proud.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "It takes courage to face an enemy. It takes more to face your friends when they are about to do something foolish. Neville Longbottom chose the harder bravery."
#### C20-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Jewels pour. Gryffindor passes Slytherin.
- **Image:** The red jewels rush. The level passes the green. It is obvious, undeniable, a little cascade. The tie breaks. The Cup’s future swings. Slytherin’s table makes a sound like a groan. Gryffindor rises without planning to.
- **Setting:** The hourglasses, the hall, banners that are green and about to be wrong.
- **Who:** The jewels, the heroes of the panel. Draco, platinum hair, goblet rigid. Snape, eyes shut. McGonagall, a real smile. Neville, Harry, Ron, Hermione on their feet or almost. Dumbledore, pleased, not gloating.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at the red glass, awe. Harry looks at Neville, not at the Cup. Draco looks at the glass, the smirk dead, pale fury. Snape looks at nothing, sour, contained. McGonagall looks at her house, fierce joy. Hermione looks at the numbers, they work, a laugh.
- **Dialogue:** **Seamus:** "We're past them! Gryffindor has passed Slytherin!"
#### C20-P3-P5

- **Beat:** The hall detonates. Neville is lifted. Hermione is crying and laughing.
- **Image:** Chaos of joy. Neville is up on shoulders or half-lifted by Dean and Seamus, terrified and laughing. Hermione has tears and a laugh at once. Ron is shouting. Harry is shouting. The twins set off something they should not. Red and gold scarves. The green banners look lonely.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall, candles swinging, the ceiling’s stars, the feast forgotten. Noise.
- **Who:** Neville aloft, sandy hair, gobletless, round face, joy. Hermione, tears, laugh, hair. Harry, glasses, scar, mouth open, cheering. Ron, red hair, bandage forgotten. The house around them. Hagrid at the doors or the staff edge, enormous, blowing his nose into a tablecloth-sized handkerchief, proud.
- **Faces:** Neville looks down at his friends, laughing, crying, the boy who fell off the broom. Hermione looks at him, crying and laughing, hands together. Harry looks up at him, the year’s kindness rewarded. Hagrid looks at Harry, then at all of them, undone. Joy, loud, earned.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Neville! You did that!" **Harry:** "Up you come, Neville!"
#### C20-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Draco still holds his goblet. It is the only still thing at his table.
- **Image:** A quiet cutaway inside the noise. Slytherin is moving, groaning, sitting down hard. Draco has not moved. The goblet is still raised from the toast that curdled. His face is still. The juice does not tremble. He looks at Harry, and Harry, in the celebration, does not look back. That is the sting.
- **Setting:** Slytherin table. Green banners. The red celebration soft in the background.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair perfect, robes, the goblet, still. Crabbe and Goyle looking at him, not knowing what face to wear. Snape seated, a hand over his eyes or just his sour mouth.
- **Faces:** Draco looks across at Harry, envy, humiliation, the year’s offer of friendship long dead. He looks alone inside his house. Harry, distant, looks at Neville, not at Draco. The goblet is the expression: a toast nobody joined. Still, small, mean, and sad.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 6 — Home, for now

#### C20-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The train home. The same compartment as September. They are not the same.
- **Image:** The scarlet train. A compartment. The same window, the same benches. September was sweets and strangers. Now there are frog cards, a chess set, Hermione’s book, Ron’s rat, Harry’s owl. The countryside runs. They fit.
- **Setting:** The Hogwarts Express, daytime, summer countryside, green and gold. The compartment, warm wood, luggage rack full.
- **Who:** Harry, Muggle clothes beginning to show under an open robe or already the too-big shirt waiting in a trunk, glasses, scar, a chocolate frog. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, Scabbers on his knee, healthier. Hermione, a book, hair, a card she is reading aloud. Hedwig’s cage, white owl, gold eyes.
- **Faces:** They look comfortable. Harry looks at Ron, mid-laugh. Ron looks at Scabbers, fond. Hermione looks at the card, then at them, a smile that includes the book and the boys. No one looks like a first-day stranger. The window shows a year gone by.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Same compartment as September. Better year." **Harry:** "Speak for Scabbers. He slept through most of it."
#### C20-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione has a book. Ron has a real sandwich. Harry has both friends.
- **Image:** A small still life that is a victory. Hermione’s book, open. Ron’s sandwich, thick, not dry, his mother’s, both hands. Harry between them, nothing in his hands for a second, looking at the sandwich and the book and the fact of them. Hedwig preens.
- **Setting:** The compartment table. Sun. Crumbs. Countryside.
- **Who:** Hermione, the book, a neat thumb. Ron, the sandwich, freckles, a bit of mustard, maroon sleeves. Harry, glasses, scar, the green Weasley sweater folded on the seat beside him, he is wearing a shirt that is almost his size. Hedwig.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the page, content, she will look up in a second. Ron looks at the sandwich, bliss, a boy with enough. Harry looks at them, quiet happiness, the cupboard far. He looks full. Not famous. Accompanied.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Mum's sandwiches. Not dry. That's how you know she missed me." **Hermione:** "And I have a book. Balance is restored."
#### C20-P4-P3

- **Beat:** They promise to write. Hedwig blinks, already drafted.
- **Image:** A pact. Hermione holds up a quill like a contract. Ron nods, mouth full, sincere. Harry puts a hand on Hedwig’s cage, she will carry it. Hedwig blinks, slow, gold eyes, she has already accepted the job. The promise is a child’s and it will hold.
- **Setting:** The compartment. The train whistle, far. Summer light.
- **Who:** The three, and Hedwig. Quill, sandwich, cage.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, earnest, a little bossy, loving. Ron looks at her, nodding, he will write badly and truly. Harry looks at Hedwig, a smile, he has a way to reach them that is not the Dursleys’ permission. Hedwig looks at him, steady, regal, fond.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "We write. Proper letters, not only at Christmas." **Harry:** "Hedwig already thinks she has the post." **Ron:** "I'll write. The spelling will be tragic. But I'll write."

#### C20-P4-P4

- **Beat:** King’s Cross. Molly hugs Harry as if he is one of hers.
- **Image:** The platform, steam, the barrier’s world. Molly Weasley, red hair, a homemade cardigan, gathers Harry in with the same arms she uses for Ron. He disappears a little. The green sweater is in his trunk because another is coming, or he is wearing it and she pats the H. Ginny peeks. Arthur shakes a hand. The Dursleys are not in this panel. This one is family.
- **Setting:** Platform 9¾, or just beyond, steam, trolleys, summer, the scarlet train. A crowd of parents.
- **Who:** Molly, plump, kind, cardigan, hugging. Harry, glasses, scar, surprised arms, the hug. Ron, grinning, freckles, suitcase. Hermione with her parents a little off, bushy hair, a wave. Ginny, small, red hair, staring at Harry with interest. Fred and George already in trouble with a trolley.
- **Faces:** Molly looks at Harry, maternal, she has decided. Harry looks over her shoulder, stunned, then he leans in, a boy learning what the hug is for. Ron looks at them, pleased with the world. Hermione looks back, a wave, soft. Ginny looks at the scar, curious, not afraid.
- **Dialogue:** **Molly:** "Harry, dear, come here. You're as thin as Ron, and that is saying something. You write to me if those Muggles are foul to you."
#### C20-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Vernon waits, stiff. Harry walks toward him without hurrying.
- **Image:** Through the barrier, the ordinary station. Vernon waits, arms folded, a car key. He must be the same man as chapters 2 and 3. The ending has been drifting. Large and beefy, very little neck, big black mustache, small angry eyes, brown business suit, white shirt, tie. If his face is flushed, the flush is temper, not a purple suit. Petunia is thin beside him. Dudley is heavier, bored, a new toy, not a tail. Harry walks toward them, trunk, cage, no hurry.
- **Setting:** King’s Cross, Muggle side. Pigeons. Announcements. Summer. No magic visible except the way Harry carries himself. Same station family as the September drop-off.
- **Who:** Vernon, the chapters 2–3 lock: large, beefy, almost no neck, big black mustache, small angry eyes, brown suit. Not a thinner man and not a new face. Petunia, thin, long neck, pale, blonde hair pinned tight, belted coat, handbag, a tight smile that is not one. Dudley, blond, round, much heavier than Harry, striped shirt or a bright jacket, bored. Harry, shirt that almost fits or the green H jumper, glasses, scar, trunk, Hedwig’s cage, calm walk.
- **Faces:** Vernon looks at Harry, a warning already, the old house, small angry eyes under the same mustache. Harry looks at Vernon, unafraid, polite, a nod. Petunia looks at the cage, distaste, and at Harry’s face, unsettled. Dudley looks at the owl, wary. Harry does not look at the floor. That is the change.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "Trunk in the boot. And no funny business until September." **Harry:** "Hello, Uncle Vernon."
#### C20-P4-P6

- **Beat:** He looks back. Ron and Hermione wave. He touches the scar, then the trunk, and goes. He is coming back.
- **Image:** The last panel. Harry at the edge of the Muggle station, one hand on the trunk, the other just leaving his scar. He looks back. Through the crowd, or through the impossible gap, Ron and Hermione are still there, waving, red hair and brown, a green sweater’s promise, a book, a rat, an owl. He looks at them. Then he looks forward, at the car, at the summer, at a return already certain. The station is ordinary. He is not only ordinary. He goes.
- **Setting:** King’s Cross, wide, light, pigeons, the Dursley car small ahead, the magic of the platform a glimmer behind. Summer. A beginning disguised as an ending.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, hand leaving his forehead, the other on the trunk, Hedwig’s cage, the green sweater visible, messy hair. Ron and Hermione in the distance, waving, smiling. Vernon at the car is the same large beefy man, very little neck, big black mustache, brown suit, not a new uncle. The crowd.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back, a real smile, small, sure. Ron looks at him, a big wave, loyal. Hermione looks at him, a wave, a see-you-soon. Vernon looks at his watch, impatient, small angry eyes, the same mustache as the early chapters. He is not the point. Harry’s face holds the year, and it is still a child’s face. He is coming back.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "See you, Harry!" **Hermione:** "Write!" **Harry:** "I will. I'm coming back."
